# The activity ledger reconciles to $68.80; the chart overstates it by $53.2874

The supplied records account for the entire latest **$68.80 cash balance**, starting from zero. There is no unexplained $17 debit in this export. The publisher's resolution-based account calculation gives **$122.0874**, or **$53.2874 too high** before any additional rounding from splitting events among bots. Its basic mistake is treating selected position-resolution P&L snapshots as a complete account ledger, while claiming that those values include fees.

The earlier app readings cannot all be reproduced as contemporaneous portfolio totals from the supplied event dates. That is a separate historical-data conflict, described below. Matching today's balance does not establish that those earlier app chart points are current, correctly dated, or measuring the same thing.

Only REPORT.md was created. No publisher execution, network connection, account action, trade, contact, or source-file edit was performed.

Thus the publisher overstates trading cash performance by $83.3574 and understates funding by $30.07. The net overstatement is $53.2874. Subtracting every fee from its existing resolution-only total would also be wrong: it omits both gains and losses from markets closed through fills.

## Funding records and their dates

Dates in this report are UTC unless explicitly marked EDT (America/Indianapolis, UTC-4 on these dates). Balance changes use createTime; later updateTime often reflects processing completion, rather than when money first became available. Deposits with advances are counted once, not again through their nested transactions.

The exact second of original bonus availability is not separately recorded.

Transfers `CSAC9NZJM00Y` (+$2.60, Sep 29) and `CXX9F1Z1G010` (+$2.47, Oct 6) have no fee description, direction field, or meaningful transfer reason. Treating their positive amounts as credits gives the exact final reconciliation. Their underlying purpose cannot be established; calling them withdrawals or fees would be unsupported. Counting them as debits would lower the ending result by $10.14.

## Running cash versus the app's known points

**Cash and portfolio total are different while positions are open.** This file supplies fills and settlement snapshots, not historical market marks. The cash ledger below is reconstructible; an exact historical portfolio-total chart is not. No value was invented for open positions. Pending-order cash reservations are also not exported here.

If the Oct 16 02:00 point was intended as UTC, reconstructed cash is $55.74955, with two football positions still open; that interpretation also fails $14.34. If Oct 18 03:25 was UTC, cash is $61.55, with football positions still open; it also fails $12.66. A timezone conversion alone does not solve these conflicts.

As a sensitivity check, delaying the $25 bonus until Oct 14 would make Oct 8 cash $24.23105, still $1.93895 below $26.17 before considering the open position. It does not establish an alternative reconciled history. Under the main ledger convention, all event-by-event cash balances are nonnegative; the minimum is $0.00375 on Sep 15 16:24:05.997984721Z (trade `CGTW192MYVAY`).

The correct conclusion is that the **latest dollars reconcile exactly, while these historical app points remain incompatible or insufficiently specified**. The supplied files cannot prove whether the problem is stale app sampling, a different chart measure, different historical credit timing, or another historical reporting issue. Do not add an artificial balancing transaction or shift the $75 to force those points to match.

## Why resolutions, fees and fills cannot be summed the old way

The export contains 1,987 top-level records: 1,709 trades, 262 resolutions, 8 deposits, 5 withdrawals, 2 transfers and 1 bonus. Trade IDs are unique; resolution (marketSlug, side) pairs and resolution market slugs are unique. There is no duplicate-resolution explanation for the $53.2874 gap.

Of 403 traded markets, 262 have a resolution record. The remaining 141 consist of **140 flat markets and one 0.01-share position**. They are not 141 missing settlement payments: positions closed through trades need no resolution entry. Their aggregate net cash is +$9.7272, including the open dust position's lifetime cash movements. Examples include the omitted Miami-Orlando and Pittsburgh-Boston College losses above.

The 262 resolved markets generated -$245.1672 net cash, comprising -$156.5972 before fill commissions and -$88.57 commissions. Their afterPosition.realized fields sum to only -$152.0826. The latter are accounting snapshots, not a fee-inclusive cash total.

A clear example is Cincinnati-West Virginia: afterPosition.realized = -$10.8632, while the actual purchase debits are $10.16325 and $0.9999, totaling **-$11.16315**, with $0.30 commissions. Fresno State similarly reports realized -$4.9540, while fill `D5Q5VTFGEZRN` (JSON record 4) debits $5.11405 including its $0.16 execution commission. Resolution is record 0, slug `aec-cfb-frest-sdst-2026-10-17`, at Oct 18 05:50:59.120079147Z.

The $4.5146 additional pre-fee discrepancy is concentrated in these snapshots. It is not an invented fee. It is the directly measured difference between a market's complete fill/payout cash result before fees and its final reported realized field:

For example, neutral UFC resolution `aec-ufc-marmcg-bensop-2026-10-03` has realized zero but pays **$1.8368**, against **$2.0508** of actual fill debits (fills `CR97RDR9YYAJ`, `CR97RDRA4YAJ`, Sep 27 06:07:04Z). Its net cash result is **-$0.2140**. Treating every resolution as a binary $0/$1 payout would miss that $1.8368 and end at $66.9632. Its stored beforePosition.cost ($2.0555) also differs from the actual fill debit sum; it is not a substitute for the cash ledger. Other snapshot inconsistencies are measured above; this export does not document the venue's internal cost-basis/reset rules sufficiently to assign a stronger cause.

Use only the owned execution selected by isAggressor. Its commissionNotionalCollected is the fee for that fill. commissionNotionalTotalCollected is a cumulative order snapshot; summing it across partial fills repeats fees. The counterparty's execution and commissions are not this account's.

There are 1,431 distinct owned order IDs; 168 orders have multiple fill records. There are 282 EXECUTION_TYPE_PARTIAL_FILL records and 1,427 EXECUTION_TYPE_FILL records. A final FILL is the final incremental execution, not a second copy of all prior shares. For example, order `CVF2CQYSMYHK` has 11 distinct fills at Oct 2 05:07:31.366330860Z; their fees sum to $4.87, the final cumulative fee. Deduplicate by fill ID, never by order ID. Use lastShares / qtyDecimal rather than integer qty or a cumulative order quantity.

There are also 50 fills that cross from a positive to a negative signed position or vice versa. Order intent/action is insufficient to compute actual debits. Fill `CVH071GWWYHR` (Oct 2 07:01:00.902387923Z, `aec-wta-taypre-diapar-2026-10-01`) says SELL_SHORT for 83.33 shares while the account is short only 14.29; it leaves **+69.04 long shares**. Treating the displayed $4.2165 cost as sale proceeds would invent cash: the correct change is **-$64.8235**. Its later $69.04 resolution payout is already in the ledger.

For signed YES position n, fill direction dq (+shares for ORDER_SIDE_BUY, -shares for ORDER_SIDE_SELL), YES price p, and signed execution fee f, the cash change is:

```
new_n = n + dq
cash_change = -dq*p - max(-new_n, 0) + max(-n, 0) - f
```

This correctly accounts for cash locked in a new short position and released when a short closes. It works across zero, unlike trusting SELL_LONG/SELL_SHORT intent labels. Settlement adds beforePosition.cashValue and sets the signed position to zero; do not add realized again. All 262 reconstructed pre-resolution decimal positions match the corresponding beforePosition.netPositionDecimal exactly.

The final dust is **+0.0100 share** of `tec-mls-eastconf-2026-12-12-w-mia`. Its last purchase cost $0.0005, and its maximum binary payout is $0.01. It cannot explain dollars of discrepancy. Historical mark-to-market changes on other open positions are not available from this export.

## The hand-versus-AI split is an estimate and can reverse who lost money

Both pm_realized_ai (lines 471-492) and pm_account_story (lines 495-547) sum **all bought and sold lastShares**, then multiply a market's final realized value by AUTOMATIC shares / all shares. The human side is its complement. The ai_by_slug argument to pm_account_story is unused. Gross traded share count is neither invested dollars nor ownership of the shares remaining at settlement.

There are 220 markets with only AUTOMATIC fills, 144 with only MANUAL fills, and 39 with both. Summing the two attribution buckets preserves the chosen market total apart from tiny rounding/threshold effects, so the split itself is not the $53.2874 account gap. It does distort performance attribution.

Cincinnati-West Virginia demonstrates this without any choice of lot accounting: both purchases lose completely. Automatic fill `D5GA3MTG2ZRM` (Oct 17 19:01:38.018720986Z) buys 15.15 shares for $10.16325 including fees. Manual fill `D5GT1JNGYZRP` (19:36:26.710544755Z) buys 187.98 shares for $0.9999 including fees. Actual losses are therefore **AI -$10.16325; hand -$0.99990**. The script instead assigns roughly **AI -$0.81021; hand -$10.05299**, before its omitted commissions. The cheap manual shares overwhelm its share-count weighting.

The existing AI headline is +$205.83984064148635 and human headline rounds to -$357.92. Neither is audited economic P&L. Bot-level splitting in apply_polymarket adds another assumption: it distributes each market's AI result by absolute desk_trades cost_usd, including fills without distinguishing entry from exit. Those desk_trades rows are not supplied, so exact per-bot results cannot be verified here. The $122.0874 baseline and interval comparisons above evaluate the supplied accounting functions with one four-decimal AI event per market. Splitting those events among multiple bots can add small rounding differences; the actual published event sum cannot be reconstructed to its final fraction of a cent without those rows or the emitted snapshot. The full cash ledger is independent of that missing allocation data.

For exact attribution, carry each opening fill's quantity, price, fee and MANUAL/AUTOMATIC owner through a stated lot convention, split crossing fills into closing and opening pieces, and assign settlement to the remaining lots. For mixed ownership, choosing FIFO versus proportional pooling is an accounting policy; the export does not identify which owner's lots a later sell consumes. Preserve an explicit mixed/unattributed bucket where the policy is not established. The account cash chart must be independent of these P&L estimates.

## Exact publisher change

Replace the chart's accounting source with a chronological cash ledger. Do not patch it with a constant -$53.2874 adjustment, subtract a guessed $17, exclude all pending deposits, or sum fees on top of an already fee-inclusive fill cash movement.

Add this pure helper immediately before pm_realized_ai. It is included here as the proposed code change; publish-crew-copy.py itself was not edited. It rejects duplicate records or incomplete position histories instead of silently manufacturing a reconciliation. It assumes this export's two completed positive TRANSFER amounts are credits and uses createTime for balance availability; those historical timing/direction limits remain explicit.

```python
def pm_cash_ledger(acts):
    """Cash only: one owned fill, resolution payout, or funding event per row.
    Amounts and quantities are USD/decimal shares; no network or publisher calls.
    """
    from collections import defaultdict
    from decimal import Decimal

    Z = Decimal("0")

    def usd(m):
        if not m or m.get("currency") != "USD":
            raise ValueError("Missing/non-USD cash value")
        return Decimal(m["value"])

    def payload(x):
        return (x.get("trade") or x.get("positionResolution")
                or x.get("accountBalanceChange"))

    def event_time(x):
        z = payload(x)
        if x.get("accountBalanceChange"):
            return z["createTime"]
        if x.get("trade"):
            e = (z["aggressorExecution"] if z["isAggressor"]
                 else z["passiveExecution"])
            return e["transactTime"]
        return z["updateTime"]

    positions = defaultdict(lambda: Z)
    market_cash = defaultdict(lambda: Z)
    owners = defaultdict(set)
    seen = set()
    events = []
    cash = funding = trading = fees = Z
    for x in sorted(acts, key=event_time):
        z = payload(x)
        at = event_time(x)
        slug = z.get("marketSlug", "")
        fee = Z
        owner = ""
        if x.get("trade"):
            rid = z["id"]
            e = (z["aggressorExecution"] if z["isAggressor"]
                 else z["passiveExecution"])
            o = e["order"]
            q = Decimal(e["lastShares"])
            side = o["side"]
            if side not in ("ORDER_SIDE_BUY", "ORDER_SIDE_SELL"):
                raise ValueError("Unknown execution side")
            dq = q if side == "ORDER_SIDE_BUY" else -q
            old = positions[slug]
            new = old + dq
            fee = usd(e["commissionNotionalCollected"])
            # YES-coordinate cash, including collateral for any SHORT position.
            delta = (-dq * usd(e["lastPx"])
                     - max(-new, Z) + max(-old, Z) - fee)
            positions[slug] = new
            flag = o.get("manualOrderIndicator", "")
            owner = ("ai" if flag == "MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_AUTOMATIC"
                     else "hand" if flag == "MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_MANUAL"
                     else "unknown")
            owners[slug].add(owner)
            kind = "trade"
            title = "Trade cash: " + slug
        elif x.get("positionResolution"):
            # Resolution has no independent ID in this export.
            rid = slug + "@" + at
            before = z["beforePosition"]
            if positions[slug] != Decimal(before["netPositionDecimal"]):
                raise ValueError("Incomplete position history: " + slug)
            # Includes NEUTRAL settlement. Do not substitute realized P&L.
            delta = usd(before["cashValue"])
            positions[slug] = Z
            kind = "payout"
            title = "Settlement cash: " + slug
        elif x.get("accountBalanceChange"):
            rid = z["transactionId"]
            status = z["status"]
            typ = x["type"]
            amount = usd(z["amount"])
            if status == "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_PENDING":
                if typ != "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT":
                    continue
                # Evidence of an advance, not a blanket acceptance of PENDING.
                advanced = {}
                for t in z.get("transactions", []):
                    if t.get("advancedAmount"):
                        advanced[t["transactionId"]] = usd(t["advancedAmount"])
                amount = min(amount, sum(advanced.values(), Z))
                if amount <= Z:
                    continue
            elif status != "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_COMPLETED":
                continue
            kinds = {
                "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT": "dep",
                "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_WITHDRAWAL": "wd",
                "ACTIVITY_TYPE_REFERRAL_BONUS": "bonus",
                "ACTIVITY_TYPE_TRANSFER": "transfer",
            }
            if typ not in kinds:
                raise ValueError("Unknown balance change type: " + typ)
            kind = kinds[typ]
            # These two TRANSFER amounts are treated as credits; reason unspecified.
            delta = -amount if kind == "wd" else amount
            title = kind + (" (advanced; pending)" if "PENDING" in status else "")
        else:
            raise ValueError("Unknown activity type")

        key = (kind, rid)
        if key in seen:
            raise ValueError("Duplicate activity; reconcile its versions: " + rid)
        seen.add(key)
        cash += delta
        fees += fee
        if kind in ("trade", "payout"):
            trading += delta
            market_cash[slug] += delta
        else:
            funding += delta
        row = {"at": at, "k": kind, "usd": float(delta),
               "usd_exact": str(delta), "cash_balance": float(cash),
               "cash_balance_exact": str(cash), "title": title,
               "record_id": rid, "slug": slug, "owner": owner,
               "fee_usd": float(fee)}
        if kind in ("dep", "wd", "bonus", "transfer"):
            row["tx"] = rid
        events.append(row)

    return {
        "events": events,
        "balance_series": [{"at": r["at"], "usd": r["cash_balance"]}
                           for r in events],
        "cash_balance": float(cash), "cash_balance_exact": str(cash),
        "net_funding": float(funding), "net_trading_cash": float(trading),
        "trade_fees": float(fees),
        "open_positions": {s: str(q) for s, q in positions.items() if q},
        "market_net_cash": {s: float(a) for s, a in market_cash.items()},
        "market_owners": {s: sorted(a) for s, a in owners.items()},
        "balance_kind": "cash", "balance_source": "activity cash ledger",
    }

```

In apply_polymarket, replace existing lines 617-618 (the apply_receipts call and the combined P&L events assignment) with the following block (leave the neighboring bot P&L calculations out of the cash series):

```python
    # Preserve the old estimates separately for inspection, not account balance.
    story["pnl_events"] = sorted(
        [e for e in story["events"] + ai_events if e["at"]],
        key=lambda e: e["at"],
    )
    ledger = pm_cash_ledger(acts)
    story["events"] = ledger["events"]
    story["bills_paid"] = apply_receipts(story["events"])
    # Keep the publisher's existing removal of transaction identifiers.
    for event in story["events"] + story["pnl_events"]:
        event.pop("tx", None)
        event.pop("record_id", None)

        "net_funding", "net_trading_cash", "trade_fees", "open_positions",
        "market_net_cash", "market_owners", "balance_kind", "balance_source",
```

The existing deposits = $318.67 and withdrawals = $44.50 fields should retain their meanings. The new net_funding field includes the $25 bonus and $5.07 transfers. ## Evidence and verification boundaries

Sources: TASK.txt's app readings; the full local polymarket-activity.json; and publish-crew-copy.py, particularly lines 471-547 and 582-627. Source data is USD. Trade times, resolution updateTime and funding createTime were ordered globally; nested execution/order/transaction copies were not counted as additional events. Arithmetic uses Python Decimal; presentation rounds only after aggregation. The proposed ledger helper and original pure accounting functions were run in isolation; the publisher's network-capable main code was never run. The source IDs and exact resolution timestamps above permit inspection without contacting any service.

Prepared by Codex. Only REPORT.md was written.
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
publish-crew.py - puts the whole AI system on heartbeatobservatory.com/live-systems/crew/ (Jaron, 2026-09-25: "everything that
people would want to see about our system. I don't want anything kept out." Secrets and keys excepted).

Every run it gathers, from the files that are actually in force: each bot's real instructions (word for word), its schedule,
its recent activity, its money (from desk_trades), the MSI's scheduled jobs, health, and the shared bot conversation log - and
writes one row to Supabase public.crew_snapshot (public read). No AI, no cost.
The named crew below is the list with its own activity source. Any other bot is added on its own when it has
state/crew/<id>.md or a grok-bot/<id> trade tag, so a new bot needs no code change. Instructions come from that
file, or from its BUILD-SHEETS section when the sheet is newer. Money is that bot's own tag.
A bot may overwrite state/crew/<id>.md (updated, what changed, then the instructions). When that file is newer than the
build sheet or script it would replace, the page uses the file. Runs every 5 minutes (task "Crew Publish", or
tools/crew-publish-loop.ps1 if that task cannot be created). The hourly MSI Health task also runs it.
Anything that looks like a key is masked before upload.
"""
import json, os, re, subprocess, sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__)))
import executor as X  # noqa: E402

WATCH = r"C:\Users\lilli\Projects\watch"
CR = os.path.join(ROOT, "projects", "control-room")

KEYLIKE = re.compile(r"(sk-[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{16,}|sb_secret_[A-Za-z0-9_\-]+|eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{20,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{10,}|"
                     r"(?i:(api[_-]?key|secret|token|password)\s*[=:]\s*)[^\s\"']{12,})")


PRIV_TAG = "[Personal chat/Secret/keys]"
EMAIL_OK = {"<email>", "<email>"}
EMAIL = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.%+-])[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9-]+(?:\.[A-Za-z0-9-]+)*\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b")
STREET = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.$#/:-])\d{1,6}\s+(?:[NSEWnsew]\.?\s+)?(?:[A-Z][A-Za-z'-]*\.?\s+){1,3}"
                    r"(?i:Dr|Drive|St|Street|Ave|Avenue|Rd|Road|Ln|Lane|Ct|Court|Blvd|Boulevard|Way|Pl|Place|Cir|Circle|Pkwy|Parkway)\b\.?")
STATES = ("AL|AK|AZ|AR|CA|CO|CT|DE|DC|FL|GA|HI|ID|IL|IN|IA|KS|KY|LA|ME|MD|MA|MI|MN|MS|MO|MT|NE|NV|NH|NJ|NM|NY|NC|ND|OH|OK|OR|"
          "PA|RI|SC|SD|TN|TX|UT|VT|VA|WA|WV|WI|WY")
ZIP = re.compile(r"(?:(?<=\b(?:" + STATES + r")\s)|(?<=\b(?:" + STATES + r"),\s)|(?<=[A-Za-z],\s))\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?(?![\w.])(?!\s*[A-Za-z(])"
                 r"|(?:(?<=\b(?:" + STATES + r")\s)|(?<=\b(?:" + STATES + r"),\s))\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?(?![\w.])")
PHONE = re.compile(r"(?<![\w$.#/:-])(?:\+?1[-. ]?)?(?:\(\d{3}\)\s?|\d{3}[-.\s])\d{3}[-.\s]\d{4}(?![\w.])")
CARD = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.$#/:-])\d{4}(?:[ -]\d{4}){2}[ -]\d{1,4}(?![\w.])|(?<![\w.$#/:-])\d{4}[ -]\d{6}[ -]\d{5}(?![\w.])")
LONGNUM = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.$/:-])\d{8,}(?![\w.])")


def privacy(text):
    """Personal bits never go public: emails, street addresses, ZIPs, phones, card and ID/confirmation numbers."""
    text = EMAIL.sub(lambda m: m.group(0) if m.group(0).lower() in EMAIL_OK else PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = STREET.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = CARD.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = PHONE.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = ZIP.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = LONGNUM.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    return text


def mask(text):
    return privacy(KEYLIKE.sub("[hidden key]", text or ""))


def mask_all(obj):
    """Run mask() over every string in the published snapshot (crew pages, build sheets, chat, activity, tasks, health)."""
    if isinstance(obj, str):
        return mask(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, list):
        return [mask_all(v) for v in obj]
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        return {k: mask_all(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
    return obj


def read(path, default=""):
    try:
        return open(path, encoding="utf-8").read()
    except OSError:
        return default


def tail(path, n):
    return [mask(l.rstrip()) for l in read(path).splitlines() if l.strip()][-n:]


def sheet_sections():
    """BUILD-SHEETS.md: section name -> instructions, routine, job, schedule."""
    text = read(os.path.join(CR, "BUILD-SHEETS.md"))
    out = {}
    for m in re.finditer(r"^# \d\. ([^\n]+)", text, re.M):
        heading = m.group(1).strip()
        name_m = re.match(r"([A-Z0-9 ]+?)(?: \(|\sâ€”|\s-|$)", heading)
        if not name_m:
            continue
        start = m.end()
        nxt = re.search(r"^# \d\. ", text[start:], re.M)
        body = text[start: start + nxt.start()] if nxt else text[start:]
        job_m = re.search(r"(?m)^\*\*Job:\*\*\s*(.+)$", body)
        sch_m = re.search(r"(?m)^\*\*Schedule:\*\*\s*(.+)$", body)
        name_line = re.search(r"(?m)^\*\*Name:\*\*\s*(.+)$", body)
        out[name_m.group(1).strip()] = {
            "blocks": re.findall(r"```\n(.*?)\n```", body, re.S),
            "job": job_m.group(1).strip() if job_m else "",
            "schedule": sch_m.group(1).strip() if sch_m else "",
            "name": name_line.group(1).strip() if name_line else "",
            "skip": bool(re.search(r"(?i)\bdelete\b", heading)),
        }
    return out


def sheet_blocks():
    """Section -> [main instructions, routine instruction] (code blocks in order)."""
    return {k: v["blocks"] for k, v in sheet_sections().items()}


def captured(name):
    """A word-for-word capture in control-room/instructions/: the text between the first two '---' lines."""
    text = read(os.path.join(CR, "instructions", name))
    m = re.search(r"\n---\n(.*?)\n---", text, re.S)
    return m.group(1).strip() if m else ""


def py_prompt(path, name):
    m = re.search(name + r' = """(.*?)"""', read(path), re.S)
    return m.group(1).strip() if m else ""


CREW = os.path.join(ROOT, "state", "crew")


def file_mtime(path):
    try:
        return os.path.getmtime(path)
    except OSError:
        return 0.0


def parse_crew(path):
    """state/crew/<id>.md written by the bot: optional header lines, then the instructions.
    Header keys: updated, changed, name, job, schedule, ai, actor, also.
    A '## Routine' heading, if present, is the routine. '## Instructions' is optional."""
    text = read(path)
    if not text.strip():
        return None
    lines = text.splitlines()
    i = 0
    meta = {}
    while i < len(lines) and not lines[i].strip():
        i += 1
    while i < len(lines):
        m = re.match(r"(?i)^(updated|changed|name|job|schedule|ai|actor|also):\s*(.*)$", lines[i])
        if not m:
            break
        meta[m.group(1).lower()] = m.group(2).strip()
        i += 1
    body = "\n".join(lines[i:]).strip()
    instructions, routine = body, None
    if re.search(r"(?m)^## (Instructions|Routine)\s*$", body):
        instructions, routine = "", None
        pre, *parts = re.split(r"(?m)^## ", body)
        if pre.strip():
            instructions = pre.strip()
        for part in parts:
            head, _, rest = part.partition("\n")
            head, rest = head.strip().lower(), rest.strip()
            if head == "instructions":
                instructions = rest
            elif head == "routine":
                routine = rest
    if not instructions.strip():
        return None
    meta.update({"instructions": instructions, "routine": routine,
                 "updated": meta.get("updated", ""), "changed": meta.get("changed", "")})
    return meta


def apply_crew(bot):
    """Prefer state/crew/<id>.md over the build sheet or script when the bot's file is newer."""
    parsed = parse_crew(os.path.join(CREW, bot["id"] + ".md"))
    if not parsed:
        return
    newest_src = max((file_mtime(p) for p in (bot.get("_src") or [])), default=0.0)
    if file_mtime(os.path.join(CREW, bot["id"] + ".md")) <= newest_src:
        return
    bot["instructions"] = parsed["instructions"]
    if parsed.get("routine"):
        bot["routine"] = [parsed["routine"]] if isinstance(bot.get("routine"), list) else parsed["routine"]
    if parsed.get("changed"):
        bot["instructions_changed"] = parsed["changed"]
    if parsed.get("updated"):
        bot["instructions_updated"] = parsed["updated"]


def drop_retired_pause(line):
    """Public session notes must not repeat the self-pause Jaron removed on 10/2.
    The log file itself is left as the bot wrote it."""
    line = line.replace("tennis ban + streak pause (Jacquet/Timofeeva)", "tennis off")
    line = line.replace("tennis ban + streak pause Jacquet/Timofeeva", "tennis off")
    line = re.sub(r"\bstreak pause\b", "no self-pause", line)
    line = re.sub(r";\s*pause(?=\))", "", line)
    line = re.sub(r"\s*\(pause\)", "", line)
    line = re.sub(r"[ \t]{2,}", " ", line)
    return line.strip()


def bot_id_of(name):
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", (name or "").lower()).strip("-")


def title_name(bid):
    return " ".join(p.capitalize() for p in bid.split("-") if p)


def one_line(text, n=180):
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text or "").strip()
    if not text:
        return ""
    m = re.match(r"(.{20,}?\.)(?:\s|$)", text)
    return (m.group(1) if m else text)[:n].rstrip()


def activity_for(bid, name):
    """The bot's own log if it has one, otherwise chat lines that name it."""
    own = tail(os.path.join(ROOT, "state", bid + ".md"), 6)
    if own:
        return own
    keys = [k for k in (bid, (name or "").lower(), "grok-bot/" + bid) if len(k) >= 4]
    hits = []
    for line in read(os.path.join(ROOT, "state", "bot-chat.md")).splitlines():
        low = line.lower()
        if any(k in low for k in keys):
            hits.append(mask(line.rstrip()))
    return hits[-6:]


def empty_ledger():
    return {"fills": 0, "spent": 0.0, "settled": 0, "pnl": 0.0, "wins": 0, "losses": 0, "last": ""}


def claimed_actors(bots):
    taken = set()
    prefixes = []
    for b in bots:
        if b.get("actor"):
            taken.add(b["actor"])
            if b["actor"] == "auto:grok-bot/live-wire":
                taken.add(b["actor"] + "#v1")
        if b.get("actor_prefix"):
            prefixes.append(b["actor_prefix"])
    return taken, prefixes


def actor_free(actor, taken, prefixes):
    if not actor or actor in taken or any(actor.startswith(p) for p in prefixes):
        return ""
    return actor


def section_for(bid, sections):
    meta = sections.get(bid.replace("-", " ").upper()) or {}
    if meta.get("skip"):
        return {}
    blocks = meta.get("blocks") or []
    return {
        "name": meta.get("name") or "",
        "job": meta.get("job") or "",
        "schedule": meta.get("schedule") or "",
        "instructions": blocks[0] if blocks else "",
        "routine": blocks[1] if len(blocks) > 1 else "",
    }


def material_for(bid, sections):
    """Crew file when it is newer than the build sheet; otherwise the sheet. Gaps fill from the other."""
    sheet = section_for(bid, sections)
    path = os.path.join(CREW, bid + ".md")
    parsed = parse_crew(path)
    crew_newer = bool(parsed) and file_mtime(path) > file_mtime(os.path.join(CR, "BUILD-SHEETS.md"))
    src = parsed if crew_newer else None

    def pick(key):
        if src and src.get(key):
            return src[key]
        return sheet.get(key) or (parsed or {}).get(key) or ""

    instructions = pick("instructions")
    routine = pick("routine") or ""
    return {
        "name": pick("name") or title_name(bid),
        "job": pick("job") or one_line(instructions) or ("Trades on Polymarket US under grok-bot/%s." % bid),
        "schedule": pick("schedule") or "see its instructions",
        "ai": pick("ai"),
        "also": pick("also"),
        "actor": (src or {}).get("actor") or "",
        "instructions": instructions,
        "routine": routine,
        "updated": ((parsed or {}).get("updated") or "") if crew_newer else "",
        "changed": ((parsed or {}).get("changed") or "") if crew_newer else "",
        "crew_newer": crew_newer,
        "parsed": parsed,
    }


def infer_actor(bid, text, hinted=""):
    if hinted:
        return hinted if hinted.startswith("auto:") else "auto:" + hinted
    tags = re.findall(r"grok-bot/([a-z0-9][a-z0-9\-]*)", text or "")
    if bid in tags:
        return "auto:grok-bot/" + bid
    if tags:
        return "auto:grok-bot/" + tags[0]
    return ""


def make_discovered(bid, sections, actor):
    fields = material_for(bid, sections)
    actor = actor_free(actor or infer_actor(bid, fields["instructions"], fields["actor"]), *claimed_holder)
    if not fields["instructions"].strip() and not actor:
        return None
    bot = {
        "id": bid,
        "name": fields["name"],
        "ai": fields["ai"] or ("Grok (xAI) - Grok Bot" if (actor or "").startswith("auto:grok-bot/") else "AI crew"),
        "job": fields["job"],
        "schedule": fields["schedule"],
        "instructions": fields["instructions"],
        "routine": [fields["routine"]] if fields["routine"] else [],
        "activity": activity_for(bid, fields["name"]),
        "money": bool(actor),
    }
    if fields["also"]:
        bot["also"] = fields["also"]
    if actor:
        bot["actor"] = actor
    if fields["crew_newer"] and fields["changed"]:
        bot["instructions_changed"] = fields["changed"]
    if fields["crew_newer"] and fields["updated"]:
        bot["instructions_updated"] = fields["updated"]
    return bot


# filled in by discover() so make_discovered can see actors already taken
claimed_holder = (set(), [])


def discover(bots, retired, per):
    """Add any bot that has state/crew/<id>.md or a grok-bot/<id> trade, and is not already on the crew."""
    global claimed_holder
    known = {b["id"] for b in bots + retired}
    taken, prefixes = claimed_actors(bots + retired)
    claimed_holder = (taken, prefixes)
    sections = sheet_sections()
    found = []

    def add(bid, actor):
        if not bid or bid in known:
            return
        bot = make_discovered(bid, sections, actor)
        if not bot:
            return
        if bot.get("actor"):
            taken.add(bot["actor"])
        found.append(bot)
        known.add(bid)

    if os.path.isdir(CREW):
        for fn in sorted(os.listdir(CREW)):
            if not fn.endswith(".md"):
                continue
            bid = fn[:-3].lower()
            if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*", bid):
                continue
            add(bid, "")

    for actor in sorted(per):
        m = re.fullmatch(r"auto:grok-bot/([a-z0-9][a-z0-9\-]*)", actor)
        if not m or not actor_free(actor, taken, prefixes):
            continue
        bid = m.group(1)
        if bid in known:
            for b in list(bots) + found:
                if b.get("id") == bid and not b.get("actor"):
                    b["actor"] = actor
                    b["money"] = True
                    taken.add(actor)
            continue
        add(bid, actor)

    idx = 0
    for i, b in enumerate(bots):
        if b.get("money") or b.get("actor") or b.get("actor_prefix"):
            idx = i + 1
    for bot in found:
        bots.insert(idx, bot)
        idx += 1


def attach_money(bots, retired, per):
    for b in bots:
        if b.get("actor"):
            hit = per.get(b["actor"])
            if hit:
                b["ledger"] = hit
            elif b.get("money"):
                b["ledger"] = empty_ledger()
        elif b.get("actor_prefix"):
            agg = {"fills": 0, "spent": 0.0, "settled": 0, "pnl": 0.0, "wins": 0, "losses": 0, "last": ""}
            for a, p in per.items():
                if a.startswith(b["actor_prefix"]):
                    for k in ("fills", "settled", "wins", "losses"):
                        agg[k] += p[k]
                    agg["spent"] = round(agg["spent"] + p["spent"], 2)
                    agg["pnl"] = round(agg["pnl"] + p["pnl"], 2)
                    agg["last"] = max(agg["last"], p["last"])
            b["ledger"] = agg
    for r in retired:   # the retired Live Wire keeps its own record (its tag's trades before the handover)
        if r.get("until"):
            r["ledger"] = per.get(r["actor"] + "#v1")


def finalize(b):
    b.pop("_src", None)
    b["instructions"] = mask(b.get("instructions", ""))
    if b.get("instructions_changed"):
        b["instructions_changed"] = mask(b["instructions_changed"])
    if isinstance(b.get("routine"), list):
        b["routine"] = [mask(x) for x in b["routine"] if x]
    else:
        b["routine"] = mask(b.get("routine", ""))


def tasks():
    ps = ("Get-ScheduledTask | Where-Object { $_.TaskPath -eq '\\' } | ForEach-Object { $i = $_ | Get-ScheduledTaskInfo; "
          "[pscustomobject]@{name=$_.TaskName; state=[string]$_.State; last=[string]$i.LastRunTime; next=[string]$i.NextRunTime; "
          "result=$i.LastTaskResult; about=$_.Description} } | ConvertTo-Json")
    try:
        d = json.loads(subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command", ps], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=90).stdout)
    except Exception:
        return []
    keep = ("Watch ", "Heartbeat News", "Trade ", "Live Board", "MSI Health", "DB Size", "Usage Snapshot", "AI-Shared Mirror",
            "Polymarket MCP", "Grok Machine MCP", "Ollama", "Scout Scorer", "Revenue Desk", "AgentWorkbench")
    return [{k: (mask(v) if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in t.items()} for t in (d if isinstance(d, list) else [d])
            if t["name"].startswith(keep)]


# Darios Twin took over from the retired Live Wire at this moment and reuses its tag: split that tag's trades here
CUT = "2026-09-25T22:58:00Z"
NOTE = ("Darios Twin replaced the retired Live Wire bot at 6:58 PM on Sept 25, 2026 - two different bots. Darios Twin reuses "
        "Live Wire's trade tag (grok-bot/live-wire) and log file (live-wire.md), so those still say live-wire. "
        "Trades before that moment are Live Wire's; trades after are Darios Twin's.")


def era(r):   # the tag's owner at the time of the row
    a = r.get("actor") or "?"
    if a == "auto:grok-bot/live-wire" and (r.get("at") or "") < CUT: return a + "#v1"
    return a


PMV = os.path.join(ROOT, "runtime", "polymarket-us-agent-venv", "Scripts", "python.exe")
PMH = os.path.join(ROOT, "state", "polymarket-activities-all.json")


def pm_history():
    """Polymarket's own full history (read-only), refreshed at most hourly. Returns the activity list or None."""
    import time
    try:
        if not os.path.exists(PMH) or time.time() - os.path.getmtime(PMH) > 3300:
            subprocess.run([PMV, os.path.join(ROOT, "tools", "pm-history-all.py")], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=240)
        return json.load(open(PMH, encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception as e:
        print("pm history unavailable:", type(e).__name__)
        return None


def pm_realized_ai(acts):
    """{market slug: AI share of Polymarket's own realized value (fees included)} - markets split by shares per manualOrderIndicator."""
    shares, real = {}, {}
    for x in acts:
        t = x.get("trade")
        if t:
            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t.get("isAggressor") else t["passiveExecution"]
            ind = (e.get("order") or {}).get("manualOrderIndicator", "")
            c = shares.setdefault(t["marketSlug"], {"ai": 0.0, "all": 0.0})
            q = float(e.get("lastShares") or 0)
            c["all"] += q
            if ind.endswith("AUTOMATIC"):
                c["ai"] += q
        r = x.get("positionResolution")
        if r:
            real[r["marketSlug"]] = float(r["afterPosition"]["realized"]["value"])
    out = {}
    for slug, v in real.items():
        c = shares.get(slug)
        if c and c["all"] > 0 and c["ai"] > 0:
            out[slug] = v * c["ai"] / c["all"]
    return out


def pm_account_story(acts, ai_by_slug):
    """The human side of the account, from Polymarket's own history (read-only): hand trades (MANUAL orders) settled P&L,
    deposits, withdrawals. Per market the human share = the shares NOT bought/sold by an AUTOMATIC order."""
    shares, real, meta = {}, {}, {}
    dep = wd = 0.0
    cash = []
    for x in acts:
        t = x.get("trade")
        if t:
            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t.get("isAggressor") else t["passiveExecution"]
            ind = (e.get("order") or {}).get("manualOrderIndicator", "")
            c = shares.setdefault(t["marketSlug"], {"ai": 0.0, "all": 0.0})
            q = float(e.get("lastShares") or 0)
            c["all"] += q
            if ind.endswith("AUTOMATIC"):
                c["ai"] += q
        r = x.get("positionResolution")
        if r:
            real[r["marketSlug"]] = float(r["afterPosition"]["realized"]["value"])
            mm = r["afterPosition"].get("marketMetadata") or {}
            meta[r["marketSlug"]] = (r.get("updateTime") or "", (r.get("market") or {}).get("question") or mm.get("title") or r["marketSlug"])
        b = x.get("accountBalanceChange")
        # 10/18: Jaron's $75 card deposit (10/15) is still "PENDING" in Polymarket's history 3 days later, yet the money is in the
        # account and trading - so a pending DEPOSIT counts (labelled pending). Pending withdrawals still wait for COMPLETED.
        st = (b or {}).get("status") or ""
        is_dep = "DEPOSIT" in x.get("type", "")
        if b and ("COMPLETED" in st or (is_dep and "PENDING" in st)):
            v = float((b.get("amount") or {}).get("value") or 0)
            kind = "dep" if is_dep else "wd" if "WITHDRAWAL" in x.get("type", "") else ""
            if kind:
                if kind == "dep": dep += v
                else: wd += v
                cash.append({"at": b.get("createTime") or "", "k": kind, "usd": round(v if kind == "dep" else -v, 4),
                             "title": ("Deposit" + (" (Polymarket still lists it as pending)" if "PENDING" in st else "")) if kind == "dep" else "Withdrawal",
                             "tx": b.get("transactionId") or ""})
    events, hand, hand_n = [], 0.0, 0
    hw = hl = 0.0
    hwn = hln = 0
    for slug, v in real.items():
        c = shares.get(slug)
        f = (c["ai"] / c["all"]) if c and c["all"] > 0 else 0.0
        h = v * (1 - f)
        if (1 - f) > 0.01 and abs(h) >= 0.005:
            hand += h
            hand_n += 1
            if h > 0: hw += h; hwn += 1
            else: hl += -h; hln += 1
            events.append({"at": meta[slug][0], "k": "hand", "usd": round(h, 4), "title": meta[slug][1]})
    events += cash
    return {"deposits": round(dep, 2), "withdrawals": round(wd, 2), "hand_result": round(hand, 2), "hand_markets": hand_n,
            "hand_won": round(hw, 2), "hand_won_n": hwn, "hand_lost": round(hl, 2), "hand_lost_n": hln,
            "events": events, "meta": meta}


def apply_receipts(events):
    """Optional receipts (public.desk_receipts): a withdrawal with a receipt becomes a 'bill' event (yellow on the page).
    Match by Polymarket transaction id, else by day + amount. Only the bill name, amount and an optional link go public."""
    try:
        env = X.secrets()
        recs = X.sb(env, "GET", "desk_receipts?select=tx_id,paid_on,amount_usd,what,receipt_url&order=id.asc&limit=500") or []
    except Exception as e:
        print("receipts unavailable:", type(e).__name__)
        return 0.0
    total, used = 0.0, set()
    for ev in events:
        if ev.get("k") != "wd":
            continue
        for i, r in enumerate(recs):
            if i in used:
                continue
            amt = float(r.get("amount_usd") or 0)
            same_tx = r.get("tx_id") and r["tx_id"] == ev.get("tx")
            same_day = (not r.get("tx_id")) and r.get("paid_on") and abs(amt - abs(ev["usd"])) < 0.005 and                 abs((datetime.fromisoformat(ev["at"][:10]) - datetime.fromisoformat(str(r["paid_on"])[:10])).days) <= 1
            if same_tx or same_day:
                used.add(i)
                ev["k"] = "bill"
                ev["bill"] = mask(str(r["what"]))[:80]
                ev["bill_usd"] = round(amt, 2)
                if r.get("receipt_url") and str(r["receipt_url"]).startswith("http"):
                    ev["receipt"] = r["receipt_url"]
                total += amt
                break
    for ev in events:
        ev.pop("tx", None)
    return round(total, 2)


def apply_polymarket(per, rows):
    """Replace each bot's settled pnl with Polymarket's realized value, attributed through desk_trades fills (by spend per market).
    Returns the headline: AI settled profit (all automatic markets), and the part no bot's fills explain."""
    acts = pm_history()
    if not acts:
        return None
    ai = pm_realized_ai(acts)
    w = {}
    for r in rows:
        if r.get("kind") == "fill" and (r.get("actor") or "").startswith("auto:") and r.get("slug") in ai:
            a = era(r)
            w.setdefault(r["slug"], {})
            w[r["slug"]][a] = w[r["slug"]].get(a, 0.0) + abs(float(r.get("cost_usd") or 0))
    for p in per.values():
        p["pnl"], p["settled"], p["wins"], p["losses"] = 0.0, 0, 0, 0
    other = 0.0
    story = pm_account_story(acts, ai)
    ai_events = []
    for slug, v in ai.items():
        ws = w.get(slug)
        tot = sum(ws.values()) if ws else 0
        at, title = story["meta"].get(slug, ("", slug))
        if not tot:
            other += v
            ai_events.append({"at": at, "k": "ai", "usd": round(v, 4), "title": title, "actor": ""})
            continue
        for a, x in ws.items():
            part = v * x / tot
            ai_events.append({"at": at, "k": "ai", "usd": round(part, 4), "title": title, "actor": a})
            per[a]["pnl"] += part
            per[a]["settled"] += 1
            per[a]["wins" if part > 0 else "losses"] += 1 if part != 0 else 0
    for p in per.values():
        p["pnl"] = round(p["pnl"], 2)
    story.pop("meta")
    story["bills_paid"] = apply_receipts(story["events"])
    story["events"] = sorted([e for e in story["events"] + ai_events if e["at"]], key=lambda e: e["at"])
    story["ai_result"] = round(sum(ai.values()), 2)
    story["ai_won"] = round(sum(v for v in ai.values() if v > 0), 2)
    story["ai_won_n"] = sum(1 for v in ai.values() if v > 0)
    story["ai_lost"] = round(-sum(v for v in ai.values() if v < 0), 2)
    story["ai_lost_n"] = sum(1 for v in ai.values() if v < 0)
    global ACCOUNT_STORY
    ACCOUNT_STORY = story
    return {"ai_settled": round(sum(ai.values()), 2), "unattributed": round(other, 2), "markets": len(ai),
            "source": "Polymarket realized per settled market, fees included"}


AI_HEAD = None
ACCOUNT_STORY = None


def money(env):
    rows = X.sb_all(env, "desk_trades?select=actor,kind,cost_usd,pnl_usd,at,slug,side")
    per = {}
    for r in rows:
        a = era(r)
        p = per.setdefault(a, {"fills": 0, "spent": 0.0, "settled": 0, "pnl": 0.0, "wins": 0, "losses": 0, "last": "", "_t": set()})
        if r.get("kind") == "fill":
            # one trade = one market + side (+ hour), however many partial fills the venue split it into
            p["_t"].add((r.get("slug"), r.get("side"), (r.get("at") or "")[:13]))
            p["spent"] += float(r.get("cost_usd") or 0)
        elif r.get("kind") == "resolution":
            v = float(r.get("pnl_usd") or 0)
            p["settled"] += 1
            p["pnl"] += v
            p["wins" if v > 0 else "losses"] += 1 if v != 0 else 0
        p["last"] = max(p["last"], r.get("at") or "")
    for p in per.values():
        p["fills"] = len(p.pop("_t"))
        p["spent"], p["pnl"] = round(p["spent"], 2), round(p["pnl"], 2)
    global AI_HEAD
    AI_HEAD = apply_polymarket(per, rows)
    return per

def desk_rules():
    """The live Control Room schedule in plain words (tools/desk.py rules): standing rule + weekly hours. Empty list if unavailable."""
    try:
        out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(ROOT, "tools", "desk.py"), "rules"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60).stdout
    except Exception:
        return []
    lines = [l.rstrip() for l in out.splitlines()]
    rules = []
    for l in lines[1:]:
        if l.startswith("No new positions"):
            rules.append(l.strip())
    if "Weekly hours:" in lines:
        i = lines.index("Weekly hours:")
        hours = []
        for l in lines[i + 1:]:
            if not l.startswith("  "):
                break
            hours.append(l.strip())
        if hours:
            rules.append("Weekly trading hours (new positions; exits always allowed): " + "; ".join(hours) + ".")
    return rules

            "Polymarket is short-term: a new trade must settle within 14 days - it pays this month's bills.",
            "Trades go through one script on the MSI that checks the book, the spread and the size, and records who decided and why.",
            "Jaron can pause any trader at any time, and has (DeepSeek on Oct 1, Demis Twin on Oct 4). The desk hours and size caps below live in the Control Room and change without a rebuild.",
            "After 2026 the system pays its own subscriptions from what it earns.",
        ] + desk_rules(),
    }
    data = mask_all(data)  # privacy filter on every published string (Jaron 2026-10-09)
    names = ", ".join(b["name"] for b in bots)
    if os.environ.get("CREW_DRY"):
        for b in bots:
            led = b.get("ledger") or {}
            print("bot", b["name"], b.get("id"), b.get("actor") or b.get("actor_prefix") or "-",
                  "instr", len(b.get("instructions") or ""), "routine", len(b.get("routine") or []),
                  "activity", len(b.get("activity") or []), "fills", led.get("fills", "-"), "pnl", led.get("pnl", "-"))
        print("dry", len(bots), "bots:", names)
        return
    body = [{"id": "current", "data": data, "updated_at": data["updated"]}]
    url = f"{env['SUPABASE_URL'].rstrip('/')}/rest/v1/crew_snapshot?on_conflict=id"
    import urllib.request
    key = env["SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"]
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=json.dumps(body).replace(chr(92) + "u0000", "").encode(), method="POST",
                                 headers={"apikey": key, "Authorization": f"Bearer {key}", "Content-Type": "application/json",
                                          "Prefer": "resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal"})
    urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=45).read()
    print("published", len(json.dumps(data)), "bytes,", len(bots), "bots,", len(data["tasks"]), "tasks")
    print("on shift:", names)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

    [(d.trading_now || []).length || d.bots.length, (d.trading_now || []).length ?     [acct ? usd(acct.buying_power) : "â€“", "cash ready"], [acct ? usd(inPos) : "â€“", "in open trades"],
  ];
  const sg = (n) => (n < 0 ? "-$" : "+$") + Math.abs(n).toFixed(2);
  if (S) {

# Public by Default

Treat the user's work as intended for public participation: visible source and a live, no-login experience that anyone can test. Local development is allowed for implementation and automated checks, but is not a complete user handoff.

## Default policy

- Make repositories public unless the user explicitly marks the work as private, deal-related, partnership-related, or restricted.
- Treat a shareable, no-login public URL as part of done for browser-based projects. Do not present localhost as the primary way for the user or public to test.
- Use localhost, dev servers, emulators, and automated tests freely during implementation.
- Do not expose secrets, personal data, API keys, or unlicensed private material. Public intent does not authorize their disclosure.
- Do not add a project-wide open-source license unless ownership/permission is clear or the user has selected it. A public repository without a license is source-visible, not legally open source.

## Choose a public delivery path

1. Deploy static HTML/CSS/JS sites to GitHub Pages when the project is already on GitHub and needs no server runtime.
2. Deploy applications needing a server, functions, previews, or framework builds to Vercel.
3. Use the user's website or custom domain when they request it or provide the integration details.
4. Keep the public experience open: no account requirement, invite gate, or login wall unless the user explicitly asks for one.

## Publish workflow

1. Build and test locally as useful.
2. Commit and publish the intended source to a public repository.
3. Configure the appropriate public host and production deployment.
4. Update README or user-facing documentation to lead with a clear live link and plain-language note that it works without installation; remove localhost-only instructions from the primary getting-started path.
5. Verify the exact public URL after deployment: request it without authentication and check that the entry page and essential assets load. Use browser testing when available.
6. Hand off with the live URL, repository URL, and the validation result.

## Exceptions and confirmation

- Respect an explicit request to keep a project private, delay release, use a login, or avoid publishing.
- If the hosting choice affects cost, a paid account, a custom-domain change, ownership permissions, or external data access, explain the decision and obtain the needed authorization.
- If a project is not web-deliverable, provide the most accessible no-login public alternative available, such as a release page or downloadable artifact, and say what public testing is possible.
