ZIP = re.compile(r"(?:(?<=\b(?:" + STATES + r")\s)|(?<=\b(?:" + STATES + r"),\s)|(?<=[A-Za-z],\s))\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?(?![\w.])(?!\s*[A-Za-z(])"
                 r"|(?:(?<=\b(?:" + STATES + r")\s)|(?<=\b(?:" + STATES + r"),\s))\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?(?![\w.])")
PHONE = re.compile(r"(?<![\w$.#/:-])(?:\+?1[-. ]?)?(?:\(\d{3}\)\s?|\d{3}[-.\s])\d{3}[-.\s]\d{4}(?![\w.])")
CARD = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.$#/:-])\d{4}(?:[ -]\d{4}){2}[ -]\d{1,4}(?![\w.])|(?<![\w.$#/:-])\d{4}[ -]\d{6}[ -]\d{5}(?![\w.])")
LONGNUM = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.$/:-])\d{8,}(?![\w.])")


def privacy(text):
    """Personal bits never go public: emails, street addresses, ZIPs, phones, card and ID/confirmation numbers."""
    text = EMAIL.sub(lambda m: m.group(0) if m.group(0).lower() in EMAIL_OK else PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = STREET.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = CARD.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = PHONE.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = ZIP.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    text = LONGNUM.sub(PRIV_TAG, text)
    return text


def mask(text):
    return privacy(KEYLIKE.sub("[hidden key]", text or ""))


def mask_all(obj):
    """Run mask() over every string in the published snapshot (crew pages, build sheets, chat, activity, tasks, health)."""
    if isinstance(obj, str):
        return mask(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, list):
        return [mask_all(v) for v in obj]
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        return {k: mask_all(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
    return obj


def read(path, default=""):
    try:
        return open(path, encoding="utf-8").read()
    except OSError:
        return default


def tail(path, n):
    return [mask(l.rstrip()) for l in read(path).splitlines() if l.strip()][-n:]


def sheet_sections():
    """BUILD-SHEETS.md: section name -> instructions, routine, job, schedule."""
    text = read(os.path.join(CR, "BUILD-SHEETS.md"))
    out = {}
    for m in re.finditer(r"^# \d\. ([^\n]+)", text, re.M):
        heading = m.group(1).strip()
        name_m = re.match(r"([A-Z0-9 ]+?)(?: \(|\sâ€”|\s-|$)", heading)
        if not name_m:
            continue
        start = m.end()
        nxt = re.search(r"^# \d\. ", text[start:], re.M)
        body = text[start: start + nxt.start()] if nxt else text[start:]
        job_m = re.search(r"(?m)^\*\*Job:\*\*\s*(.+)$", body)
        sch_m = re.search(r"(?m)^\*\*Schedule:\*\*\s*(.+)$", body)
        name_line = re.search(r"(?m)^\*\*Name:\*\*\s*(.+)$", body)
        out[name_m.group(1).strip()] = {
            "blocks": re.findall(r"```\n(.*?)\n```", body, re.S),
            "job": job_m.group(1).strip() if job_m else "",
            "schedule": sch_m.group(1).strip() if sch_m else "",
def make_discovered(bid, sections, actor):
    fields = material_for(bid, sections)
    actor = actor_free(actor or infer_actor(bid, fields["instructions"], fields["actor"]), *claimed_holder)
    if not fields["instructions"].strip() and not actor:
        return None
    bot = {
        "id": bid,
        "name": fields["name"],
        "ai": fields["ai"] or ("Grok (xAI) - Grok Bot" if (actor or "").startswith("auto:grok-bot/") else "AI crew"),
        "job": fields["job"],
        "schedule": fields["schedule"],
        "instructions": fields["instructions"],
        "routine": [fields["routine"]] if fields["routine"] else [],
        "activity": activity_for(bid, fields["name"]),
        "money": bool(actor),
    }
    if fields["also"]:
        bot["also"] = fields["also"]
    if actor:
        bot["actor"] = actor
    if fields["crew_newer"] and fields["changed"]:
        bot["instructions_changed"] = fields["changed"]
    if fields["crew_newer"] and fields["updated"]:
        bot["instructions_updated"] = fields["updated"]
    return bot


# filled in by discover() so make_discovered can see actors already taken
claimed_holder = (set(), [])


def discover(bots, retired, per):
    """Add any bot that has state/crew/<id>.md or a grok-bot/<id> trade, and is not already on the crew."""
    global claimed_holder
    known = {b["id"] for b in bots + retired}
    taken, prefixes = claimed_actors(bots + retired)
    claimed_holder = (taken, prefixes)
    sections = sheet_sections()
    found = []

    def add(bid, actor):
        if not bid or bid in known:
            return
        bot = make_discovered(bid, sections, actor)
        if not bot:
            return
        if bot.get("actor"):
            taken.add(bot["actor"])
        found.append(bot)
        known.add(bid)

    if os.path.isdir(CREW):
        for fn in sorted(os.listdir(CREW)):
            if not fn.endswith(".md"):
                continue
            bid = fn[:-3].lower()
            if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*", bid):
                continue
            add(bid, "")

    for actor in sorted(per):
        m = re.fullmatch(r"auto:grok-bot/([a-z0-9][a-z0-9\-]*)", actor)
        if not m or not actor_free(actor, taken, prefixes):
            continue
        bid = m.group(1)
        if bid in known:
            for b in list(bots) + found:
                if b.get("id") == bid and not b.get("actor"):
                    b["actor"] = actor
                    b["money"] = True
                    taken.add(actor)
            continue
        add(bid, actor)

    idx = 0
    for i, b in enumerate(bots):
        if b.get("money") or b.get("actor") or b.get("actor_prefix"):
            idx = i + 1
    for bot in found:
        bots.insert(idx, bot)
        idx += 1


def attach_money(bots, retired, per):
    for b in bots:
        if b.get("actor"):
            hit = per.get(b["actor"])
            if hit:
                b["ledger"] = hit
            elif b.get("money"):
                b["ledger"] = empty_ledger()
        elif b.get("actor_prefix"):
            agg = {"fills": 0, "spent": 0.0, "settled": 0, "pnl": 0.0, "wins": 0, "losses": 0, "last": ""}
            for a, p in per.items():
                if a.startswith(b["actor_prefix"]):
                    for k in ("fills", "settled", "wins", "losses"):
                        agg[k] += p[k]
                    agg["spent"] = round(agg["spent"] + p["spent"], 2)
                    agg["pnl"] = round(agg["pnl"] + p["pnl"], 2)
                    agg["last"] = max(agg["last"], p["last"])
            b["ledger"] = agg
    for r in retired:   # the retired Live Wire keeps its own record (its tag's trades before the handover)
        if r.get("until"):
            r["ledger"] = per.get(r["actor"] + "#v1")


def finalize(b):
    b.pop("_src", None)
    b["instructions"] = mask(b.get("instructions", ""))
    if b.get("instructions_changed"):
        b["instructions_changed"] = mask(b["instructions_changed"])
    if isinstance(b.get("routine"), list):
        b["routine"] = [mask(x) for x in b["routine"] if x]
    else:
        b["routine"] = mask(b.get("routine", ""))


def tasks():
    ps = ("Get-ScheduledTask | Where-Object { $_.TaskPath -eq '\\' } | ForEach-Object { $i = $_ | Get-ScheduledTaskInfo; "
          "[pscustomobject]@{name=$_.TaskName; state=[string]$_.State; last=[string]$i.LastRunTime; next=[string]$i.NextRunTime; "
          "result=$i.LastTaskResult; about=$_.Description} } | ConvertTo-Json")
    try:
        d = json.loads(subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command", ps], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=90).stdout)
    except Exception:
        return []
    keep = ("Watch ", "Heartbeat News", "Trade ", "Live Board", "MSI Health", "DB Size", "Usage Snapshot", "AI-Shared Mirror",
            "Polymarket MCP", "Grok Machine MCP", "Ollama", "Scout Scorer", "Revenue Desk", "AgentWorkbench")
    return [{k: (mask(v) if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in t.items()} for t in (d if isinstance(d, list) else [d])
            if t["name"].startswith(keep)]


# Darios Twin took over from the retired Live Wire at this moment and reuses its tag: split that tag's trades here
CUT = "2026-09-25T22:58:00Z"
NOTE = ("Darios Twin replaced the retired Live Wire bot at 6:58 PM on Sept 25, 2026 - two different bots. Darios Twin reuses "
        "Live Wire's trade tag (grok-bot/live-wire) and log file (live-wire.md), so those still say live-wire. "
        "Trades before that moment are Live Wire's; trades after are Darios Twin's.")


def era(r):   # the tag's owner at the time of the row
    a = r.get("actor") or "?"
    if a == "auto:grok-bot/live-wire" and (r.get("at") or "") < CUT: return a + "#v1"
    return a


PMV = os.path.join(ROOT, "runtime", "polymarket-us-agent-venv", "Scripts", "python.exe")
PMH = os.path.join(ROOT, "state", "polymarket-activities-all.json")


def pm_history():
    """Polymarket's own full history (read-only), refreshed at most hourly. Returns the activity list or None."""
    import time
    try:
        if not os.path.exists(PMH) or time.time() - os.path.getmtime(PMH) > 3300:
            subprocess.run([PMV, os.path.join(ROOT, "tools", "pm-history-all.py")], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=240)
        return json.load(open(PMH, encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception as e:
        print("pm history unavailable:", type(e).__name__)
        return None


def pm_realized_ai(acts):
    """{market slug: AI share of Polymarket's own realized value (fees included)} - markets split by shares per manualOrderIndicator."""
    shares, real = {}, {}
    for x in acts:
        t = x.get("trade")
        if t:
            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t.get("isAggressor") else t["passiveExecution"]
            ind = (e.get("order") or {}).get("manualOrderIndicator", "")
            c = shares.setdefault(t["marketSlug"], {"ai": 0.0, "all": 0.0})
            q = float(e.get("lastShares") or 0)
            c["all"] += q
            if ind.endswith("AUTOMATIC"):
                c["ai"] += q
        r = x.get("positionResolution")
        if r:
            real[r["marketSlug"]] = float(r["afterPosition"]["realized"]["value"])
    out = {}
    for slug, v in real.items():
        c = shares.get(slug)
        if c and c["all"] > 0 and c["ai"] > 0:
            out[slug] = v * c["ai"] / c["all"]
    return out


def pm_account_story(acts, ai_by_slug):
    """The human side of the account, from Polymarket's own history (read-only): hand trades (MANUAL orders) settled P&L,
    deposits, withdrawals. Per market the human share = the shares NOT bought/sold by an AUTOMATIC order."""
    shares, real, meta = {}, {}, {}
    dep = wd = 0.0
    cash = []
    for x in acts:
        t = x.get("trade")
        if t:
            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t.get("isAggressor") else t["passiveExecution"]
            ind = (e.get("order") or {}).get("manualOrderIndicator", "")
            c = shares.setdefault(t["marketSlug"], {"ai": 0.0, "all": 0.0})
            q = float(e.get("lastShares") or 0)
            c["all"] += q
            if ind.endswith("AUTOMATIC"):
                c["ai"] += q
        r = x.get("positionResolution")
        if r:
            real[r["marketSlug"]] = float(r["afterPosition"]["realized"]["value"])
            mm = r["afterPosition"].get("marketMetadata") or {}
            meta[r["marketSlug"]] = (r.get("updateTime") or "", (r.get("market") or {}).get("question") or mm.get("title") or r["marketSlug"])
        b = x.get("accountBalanceChange")
        # 10/18: Jaron's $75 card deposit (10/15) is still "PENDING" in Polymarket's history 3 days later, yet the money is in the
        # account and trading - so a pending DEPOSIT counts (labelled pending). Pending withdrawals still wait for COMPLETED.
        st = (b or {}).get("status") or ""
        is_dep = "DEPOSIT" in x.get("type", "")
        if b and ("COMPLETED" in st or (is_dep and "PENDING" in st)):
            v = float((b.get("amount") or {}).get("value") or 0)
            kind = "dep" if is_dep else "wd" if "WITHDRAWAL" in x.get("type", "") else ""
            if kind:
                if kind == "dep": dep += v
                else: wd += v
                cash.append({"at": b.get("createTime") or "", "k": kind, "usd": round(v if kind == "dep" else -v, 4),
                             "title": ("Deposit" + (" (Polymarket still lists it as pending)" if "PENDING" in st else "")) if kind == "dep" else "Withdrawal",
                             "tx": b.get("transactionId") or ""})
    events, hand, hand_n = [], 0.0, 0
    hw = hl = 0.0
    hwn = hln = 0
    for slug, v in real.items():
        c = shares.get(slug)
        f = (c["ai"] / c["all"]) if c and c["all"] > 0 else 0.0
        h = v * (1 - f)
        if (1 - f) > 0.01 and abs(h) >= 0.005:
            hand += h
            hand_n += 1
            if h > 0: hw += h; hwn += 1
            else: hl += -h; hln += 1
            events.append({"at": meta[slug][0], "k": "hand", "usd": round(h, 4), "title": meta[slug][1]})
    events += cash
    return {"deposits": round(dep, 2), "withdrawals": round(wd, 2), "hand_result": round(hand, 2), "hand_markets": hand_n,
            "hand_won": round(hw, 2), "hand_won_n": hwn, "hand_lost": round(hl, 2), "hand_lost_n": hln,
            "events": events, "meta": meta}


def apply_receipts(events):
    """Optional receipts (public.desk_receipts): a withdrawal with a receipt becomes a 'bill' event (yellow on the page).
    Match by Polymarket transaction id, else by day + amount. Only the bill name, amount and an optional link go public."""
    try:
        env = X.secrets()
        recs = X.sb(env, "GET", "desk_receipts?select=tx_id,paid_on,amount_usd,what,receipt_url&order=id.asc&limit=500") or []
    except Exception as e:
        print("receipts unavailable:", type(e).__name__)
        return 0.0
    total, used = 0.0, set()
    for ev in events:
        if ev.get("k") != "wd":
            continue
        for i, r in enumerate(recs):
            if i in used:
                continue
            amt = float(r.get("amount_usd") or 0)
            same_tx = r.get("tx_id") and r["tx_id"] == ev.get("tx")
            same_day = (not r.get("tx_id")) and r.get("paid_on") and abs(amt - abs(ev["usd"])) < 0.005 and                 abs((datetime.fromisoformat(ev["at"][:10]) - datetime.fromisoformat(str(r["paid_on"])[:10])).days) <= 1
            if same_tx or same_day:
                used.add(i)
                ev["k"] = "bill"
                ev["bill"] = mask(str(r["what"]))[:80]
                ev["bill_usd"] = round(amt, 2)
                if r.get("receipt_url") and str(r["receipt_url"]).startswith("http"):
                    ev["receipt"] = r["receipt_url"]
                total += amt
                break
    for ev in events:
        ev.pop("tx", None)
    return round(total, 2)


def apply_polymarket(per, rows):
    """Replace each bot's settled pnl with Polymarket's realized value, attributed through desk_trades fills (by spend per market).
    Returns the headline: AI settled profit (all automatic markets), and the part no bot's fills explain."""
    acts = pm_history()
    if not acts:
        return None
    ai = pm_realized_ai(acts)
    w = {}
    for r in rows:
        if r.get("kind") == "fill" and (r.get("actor") or "").startswith("auto:") and r.get("slug") in ai:
            a = era(r)
            w.setdefault(r["slug"], {})
            w[r["slug"]][a] = w[r["slug"]].get(a, 0.0) + abs(float(r.get("cost_usd") or 0))
    for p in per.values():
        p["pnl"], p["settled"], p["wins"], p["losses"] = 0.0, 0, 0, 0
    other = 0.0
    story = pm_account_story(acts, ai)
    ai_events = []
    for slug, v in ai.items():
        ws = w.get(slug)
        tot = sum(ws.values()) if ws else 0
        at, title = story["meta"].get(slug, ("", slug))
        if not tot:
            other += v
            ai_events.append({"at": at, "k": "ai", "usd": round(v, 4), "title": title, "actor": ""})
            continue
        for a, x in ws.items():
            part = v * x / tot
            ai_events.append({"at": at, "k": "ai", "usd": round(part, 4), "title": title, "actor": a})
            per[a]["pnl"] += part
            per[a]["settled"] += 1
            per[a]["wins" if part > 0 else "losses"] += 1 if part != 0 else 0
    for p in per.values():
        p["pnl"] = round(p["pnl"], 2)

def apply_polymarket(per, rows):
    """Replace each bot's settled pnl with Polymarket's realized value, attributed through desk_trades fills (by spend per market).
    Returns the headline: AI settled profit (all automatic markets), and the part no bot's fills explain."""
    acts = pm_history()
    if not acts:
        return None
    ai = pm_realized_ai(acts)
    w = {}
    for r in rows:
        if r.get("kind") == "fill" and (r.get("actor") or "").startswith("auto:") and r.get("slug") in ai:
            a = era(r)
            w.setdefault(r["slug"], {})
            w[r["slug"]][a] = w[r["slug"]].get(a, 0.0) + abs(float(r.get("cost_usd") or 0))
    for p in per.values():
        p["pnl"], p["settled"], p["wins"], p["losses"] = 0.0, 0, 0, 0
    other = 0.0
    story = pm_account_story(acts, ai)
    ai_events = []
    for slug, v in ai.items():
        ws = w.get(slug)
        tot = sum(ws.values()) if ws else 0
        at, title = story["meta"].get(slug, ("", slug))
        if not tot:
            other += v
            ai_events.append({"at": at, "k": "ai", "usd": round(v, 4), "title": title, "actor": ""})
            continue
        for a, x in ws.items():
            part = v * x / tot
            ai_events.append({"at": at, "k": "ai", "usd": round(part, 4), "title": title, "actor": a})
            per[a]["pnl"] += part
            per[a]["settled"] += 1
            per[a]["wins" if part > 0 else "losses"] += 1 if part != 0 else 0
    for p in per.values():
        p["pnl"] = round(p["pnl"], 2)
    story.pop("meta")
    story["bills_paid"] = apply_receipts(story["events"])
    story["events"] = sorted([e for e in story["events"] + ai_events if e["at"]], key=lambda e: e["at"])
    story["ai_result"] = round(sum(ai.values()), 2)
    story["ai_won"] = round(sum(v for v in ai.values() if v > 0), 2)
    story["ai_won_n"] = sum(1 for v in ai.values() if v > 0)
    story["ai_lost"] = round(-sum(v for v in ai.values() if v < 0), 2)
    story["ai_lost_n"] = sum(1 for v in ai.values() if v < 0)
    global ACCOUNT_STORY
    ACCOUNT_STORY = story
    return {"ai_settled": round(sum(ai.values()), 2), "unattributed": round(other, 2), "markets": len(ai),
            "source": "Polymarket realized per settled market, fees included"}


AI_HEAD = None
ACCOUNT_STORY = None


def money(env):
    rows = X.sb_all(env, "desk_trades?select=actor,kind,cost_usd,pnl_usd,at,slug,side")
    per = {}
    for r in rows:
        a = era(r)
        p = per.setdefault(a, {"fills": 0, "spent": 0.0, "settled": 0, "pnl": 0.0, "wins": 0, "losses": 0, "last": "", "_t": set()})
        if r.get("kind") == "fill":
            # one trade = one market + side (+ hour), however many partial fills the venue split it into
            p["_t"].add((r.get("slug"), r.get("side"), (r.get("at") or "")[:13]))
            p["spent"] += float(r.get("cost_usd") or 0)
        elif r.get("kind") == "resolution":
            v = float(r.get("pnl_usd") or 0)
            p["settled"] += 1
            p["pnl"] += v
            p["wins" if v > 0 else "losses"] += 1 if v != 0 else 0
        p["last"] = max(p["last"], r.get("at") or "")
    for p in per.values():
        p["fills"] = len(p.pop("_t"))
        p["spent"], p["pnl"] = round(p["spent"], 2), round(p["pnl"], 2)
    global AI_HEAD
    AI_HEAD = apply_polymarket(per, rows)
    return per

160:Both pm_realized_ai (lines 471-492) and pm_account_story (lines 495-547) sum **all bought and sold lastShares**, then multiply a market's final realized value by AUTOMATIC shares / all shares. The human side is its complement. The ai_by_slug argument to pm_account_story is unused. Gross traded share count is neither invested dollars nor ownership of the shares remaining at settlement.
168:For exact attribution, carry each opening fill's quantity, price, fee and MANUAL/AUTOMATIC owner through a stated lot convention, split crossing fills into closing and opening pieces, and assign settlement to the remaining lots. For mixed ownership, choosing FIFO versus proportional pooling is an accounting policy; the export does not identify which owner's lots a later sell consumes. Preserve an explicit mixed/unattributed bucket where the policy is not established. The account cash chart must be independent of these P&L estimates.

    Z = Decimal("0")

    def usd(m):
        if not m or m.get("currency") != "USD":
            raise ValueError("Missing/non-USD cash value")
        return Decimal(m["value"])

    def payload(x):
        return (x.get("trade") or x.get("positionResolution")
                or x.get("accountBalanceChange"))

    def event_time(x):
        z = payload(x)
        if x.get("accountBalanceChange"):
            return z["createTime"]
        if x.get("trade"):
            e = (z["aggressorExecution"] if z["isAggressor"]
                 else z["passiveExecution"])
            return e["transactTime"]
        return z["updateTime"]

    positions = defaultdict(lambda: Z)
    market_cash = defaultdict(lambda: Z)
    owners = defaultdict(set)
    seen = set()
    events = []
    cash = funding = trading = fees = Z
    for x in sorted(acts, key=event_time):
        z = payload(x)
        at = event_time(x)
        slug = z.get("marketSlug", "")
        fee = Z
        owner = ""
        if x.get("trade"):
            rid = z["id"]
            e = (z["aggressorExecution"] if z["isAggressor"]
                 else z["passiveExecution"])
            o = e["order"]
            q = Decimal(e["lastShares"])
            side = o["side"]
            if side not in ("ORDER_SIDE_BUY", "ORDER_SIDE_SELL"):
                raise ValueError("Unknown execution side")
            dq = q if side == "ORDER_SIDE_BUY" else -q
            old = positions[slug]
            new = old + dq
            fee = usd(e["commissionNotionalCollected"])
            # YES-coordinate cash, including collateral for any SHORT position.
            delta = (-dq * usd(e["lastPx"])
                     - max(-new, Z) + max(-old, Z) - fee)
            positions[slug] = new
            flag = o.get("manualOrderIndicator", "")
            owner = ("ai" if flag == "MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_AUTOMATIC"
                     else "hand" if flag == "MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_MANUAL"
                     else "unknown")
            owners[slug].add(owner)
            kind = "trade"
            title = "Trade cash: " + slug
        elif x.get("positionResolution"):
            # Resolution has no independent ID in this export.
            rid = slug + "@" + at
            before = z["beforePosition"]
            if positions[slug] != Decimal(before["netPositionDecimal"]):
                raise ValueError("Incomplete position history: " + slug)
            # Includes NEUTRAL settlement. Do not substitute realized P&L.
            delta = usd(before["cashValue"])
            positions[slug] = Z
            kind = "payout"
            title = "Settlement cash: " + slug
        elif x.get("accountBalanceChange"):
            rid = z["transactionId"]
            status = z["status"]
            typ = x["type"]
            amount = usd(z["amount"])
            if status == "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_PENDING":
                if typ != "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT":
                    continue
                # Evidence of an advance, not a blanket acceptance of PENDING.
                advanced = {}
                for t in z.get("transactions", []):
                    if t.get("advancedAmount"):
                        advanced[t["transactionId"]] = usd(t["advancedAmount"])
                amount = min(amount, sum(advanced.values(), Z))
                if amount <= Z:
                    continue
            elif status != "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_COMPLETED":
                continue
            kinds = {
                "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT": "dep",
                "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_WITHDRAWAL": "wd",
                "ACTIVITY_TYPE_REFERRAL_BONUS": "bonus",
                "ACTIVITY_TYPE_TRANSFER": "transfer",
            }
            if typ not in kinds:
                raise ValueError("Unknown balance change type: " + typ)
            kind = kinds[typ]
            # These two TRANSFER amounts are treated as credits; reason unspecified.
            delta = -amount if kind == "wd" else amount
            title = kind + (" (advanced; pending)" if "PENDING" in status else "")
        else:
            raise ValueError("Unknown activity type")

        key = (kind, rid)
        if key in seen:
            raise ValueError("Duplicate activity; reconcile its versions: " + rid)
        seen.add(key)
        cash += delta
        fees += fee
        if kind in ("trade", "payout"):
            trading += delta
            market_cash[slug] += delta
        else:
            funding += delta
        row = {"at": at, "k": kind, "usd": float(delta),
               "usd_exact": str(delta), "cash_balance": float(cash),
               "cash_balance_exact": str(cash), "title": title,
               "record_id": rid, "slug": slug, "owner": owner,
               "fee_usd": float(fee)}
        if kind in ("dep", "wd", "bonus", "transfer"):
            row["tx"] = rid
        events.append(row)

    return {
        "events": events,
        "balance_series": [{"at": r["at"], "usd": r["cash_balance"]}
                           for r in events],
        "cash_balance": float(cash), "cash_balance_exact": str(cash),
        "net_funding": float(funding), "net_trading_cash": float(trading),
        "trade_fees": float(fees),
        "open_positions": {s: str(q) for s, q in positions.items() if q},
        "market_net_cash": {s: float(a) for s, a in market_cash.items()},
        "market_owners": {s: sorted(a) for s, a in owners.items()},
        "balance_kind": "cash", "balance_source": "activity cash ledger",
    }

+            old = positions.get(slug, zero)

+            fee = usd(e["commissionNotionalCollected"])
+            # Signed YES coordinates: lock/release SHORT collateral, even across zero.
+            delta = -dq * usd(e["lastPx"]) - max(-new, zero) + max(-old, zero) - fee
+            allocations[owner] = delta  # Every fill's cash, including its fee, belongs to its actor.
+            owner_fees[owner] += fee
+            charges += max(fee, zero)
+            rebates += max(-fee, zero)
+            pool = pools.setdefault(slug, {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)})
+            # Mixed-market convention: closing fills consume remaining shares proportionally
+            # from both owners. Only the excess of a crossing fill opens shares for its actor.
+            # Settlement pays the resulting remaining-share pool, never gross traded volume.
+            if old * dq < zero:
+                closing = min(abs(old), q)
+                remaining = Fraction(abs(old) - closing) / Fraction(abs(old))
+                pool["ai"] *= remaining
+                pool["hand"] *= remaining
+                opening = q - closing
+            else:
+                opening = q
+            pool[owner] += Fraction(opening)
+            positions[slug] = new
+            title = (t.get("market") or {}).get("question") or (order.get("marketMetadata") or {}).get("title") or slug

+                # Round only the ownership allocation to 1e-8 USD. The hand complement

+                if pool["ai"]:

+            pools[slug] = {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)}

+        elif x.get("accountBalanceChange"):

+                raise ValueError("Unknown funding type: " + x["type"])
+            amount, status = usd(b["amount"]), b["status"]
+            pending = status == "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_PENDING"

+            if pending:

+        else:
+            raise ValueError("Unknown activity type")
+        key = (kind, rid)

+            raise ValueError("Duplicate activity; reconcile its versions: " + rid)
+        seen.add(key)
+        if slug:
+            market = results.setdefault(slug, {})

+           "funding_in": float(net_funding + funding["wd"]), "withdrawals": float(funding["wd"]),

+        out[owner + "_fees_paid"] = float(owner_fees[owner])
+        out[owner + "_markets"] = len(values)

+        out[owner + "_won_n"] = sum(v > zero for v in closed)
+        out[owner + "_lost_n"] = sum(v < zero for v in closed)

+    return out

+def pm_account_story(acts, ai_by_slug=None):
+    """Account and owner cash results; the legacy ai_by_slug estimate is no longer used."""
+    return pm_cash_ledger(acts)
+
+
+def pm_cash_ledger(acts):
+    """Replay actual USD movements, independently of realized-P&L snapshots or bot estimates."""
+    zero = Decimal(0)
+
+    def usd(value):
+        if not value or value.get("currency") != "USD":
+            raise ValueError("Missing/non-USD cash value")
+        return Decimal(value["value"])
+
+    def event_time(x):
+        if x.get("trade"):
+            t = x["trade"]
+            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t["isAggressor"] else t["passiveExecution"]
+            return e["transactTime"]
+        if x.get("positionResolution"):
+            return x["positionResolution"]["updateTime"]
+        return x["accountBalanceChange"]["createTime"]
+
+    def time_key(x):
+        # Preserve nanoseconds, including when an ISO timestamp has no fractional part.
+        at = event_time(x).removesuffix("Z")
+        seconds, _, fraction = at.partition(".")
+        return seconds, fraction.ljust(9, "0")
 
+    positions, pools, results, meta = {}, {}, {}, {}
+    owner_fees = {"ai": zero, "hand": zero}
+    funding = dict.fromkeys(("dep", "wd", "bonus", "transfer"), zero)
+    cash = charges = rebates = zero
+    seen, events = set(), []
+    for x in sorted(acts, key=time_key):
+        at, fee, allocations = event_time(x), zero, {}
+        slug, tx = "", ""
+        if x.get("trade"):
+            t = x["trade"]
+            slug, rid, kind = t["marketSlug"], t["id"], "fill"
+            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t["isAggressor"] else t["passiveExecution"]
+            order = e["order"]
+            owners = {"MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_AUTOMATIC": "ai", "MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_MANUAL": "hand"}
+            if order.get("manualOrderIndicator") not in owners:
+                raise ValueError("Unknown fill owner: " + rid)
+            owner = owners[order["manualOrderIndicator"]]
+            q = Decimal(e.get("lastShares") or t["qtyDecimal"])
+            if order["side"] not in ("ORDER_SIDE_BUY", "ORDER_SIDE_SELL") or q <= zero:
+                raise ValueError("Invalid fill side/quantity: " + rid)
+            dq = q if order["side"] == "ORDER_SIDE_BUY" else -q
+            old = positions.get(slug, zero)
+            new = old + dq
+            fee = usd(e["commissionNotionalCollected"])
+            # Signed YES coordinates: lock/release SHORT collateral, even across zero.
+            delta = -dq * usd(e["lastPx"]) - max(-new, zero) + max(-old, zero) - fee
+            allocations[owner] = delta  # Every fill's cash, including its fee, belongs to its actor.
+            owner_fees[owner] += fee
+            charges += max(fee, zero)
+            rebates += max(-fee, zero)
+            pool = pools.setdefault(slug, {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)})
+            # Mixed-market convention: closing fills consume remaining shares proportionally
+            # from both owners. Only the excess of a crossing fill opens shares for its actor.
+            # Settlement pays the resulting remaining-share pool, never gross traded volume.
+            if old * dq < zero:
+                closing = min(abs(old), q)
+                remaining = Fraction(abs(old) - closing) / Fraction(abs(old))
+                pool["ai"] *= remaining
+                pool["hand"] *= remaining
+                opening = q - closing
+            else:
+                opening = q
+            pool[owner] += Fraction(opening)
+            positions[slug] = new
+            title = (t.get("market") or {}).get("question") or (order.get("marketMetadata") or {}).get("title") or slug
+        elif x.get("positionResolution"):
+            r = x["positionResolution"]
+            slug, kind = r["marketSlug"], "payout"
+            rid = slug + "@" + at
+            before = r["beforePosition"]
+            if positions.get(slug, zero) != Decimal(before["netPositionDecimal"]):
+                raise ValueError("Incomplete position history: " + slug)
+            delta = usd(before["cashValue"])  # Includes neutral payouts; realized is not cash.
+            pool = pools.get(slug, {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)})
+            total = sum(pool.values())
+            if not total and delta:
+                raise ValueError("Payout without owned shares: " + slug)
+            if total:
+                ratio = pool["ai"] / total
+                # Round only the ownership allocation to 1e-8 USD. The hand complement
+                # preserves the actual payout exactly, including repeating pool fractions.
+                ai_cash = (delta * Decimal(ratio.numerator) / Decimal(ratio.denominator)).quantize(Decimal("0.00000001"))
+                if pool["ai"]:
+                    allocations["ai"] = ai_cash
+                if pool["hand"]:
+                    allocations["hand"] = delta - ai_cash
+            positions[slug] = zero
+            pools[slug] = {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)}
+            title = (r.get("market") or {}).get("question") or (before.get("marketMetadata") or {}).get("title") or slug
+        elif x.get("accountBalanceChange"):
+            b = x["accountBalanceChange"]
+            rid = tx = b["transactionId"]
+            kind = {"ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT": "dep", "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_WITHDRAWAL": "wd",
+                    "ACTIVITY_TYPE_REFERRAL_BONUS": "bonus", "ACTIVITY_TYPE_TRANSFER": "transfer"}.get(x["type"])
+            if not kind:
+                raise ValueError("Unknown funding type: " + x["type"])
+            amount, status = usd(b["amount"]), b["status"]
+            pending = status == "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_PENDING"
+            if pending and kind == "dep":
+                advances = {t["transactionId"]: usd(t["advancedAmount"]) for t in b.get("transactions", []) if t.get("advancedAmount")}
+                amount = min(amount, sum(advances.values(), zero))
+                if amount <= zero:
+                    continue
+            elif status != "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_COMPLETED":
+                continue
+            # TRANSFER amounts in this export are signed credits; purpose is unspecified.
+            delta = -amount if kind == "wd" else amount
+            funding[kind] += amount
+            title = {"dep": "Deposit", "wd": "Withdrawal", "bonus": "Promotion bonus", "transfer": "Transfer credit"}[kind]
+            if pending:
+                title += " (advanced funds; deposit pending)"
+        else:
+            raise ValueError("Unknown activity type")
+        key = (kind, rid)
+        if key in seen:
+            raise ValueError("Duplicate activity; reconcile its versions: " + rid)
+        seen.add(key)
+        if slug:
+            market = results.setdefault(slug, {})
+            for owner, value in allocations.items():
+                market[owner] = market.get(owner, zero) + value
+            meta[slug] = (at, title)
+        cash += delta
+        ev = {"at": at, "k": kind, "usd": float(delta), "usd_exact": str(delta),
+              "cash_balance": float(cash), "cash_balance_exact": str(cash), "title": title,
+              "slug": slug, "fee_usd": float(fee), "owners": {o: float(v) for o, v in allocations.items()}}
+        if tx:
+            ev["tx"] = tx
+        events.append(ev)

+def pm_cash_ledger(acts):
+    """Replay actual USD movements, independently of realized-P&L snapshots or bot estimates."""
+    zero = Decimal(0)
+
+    def usd(value):
+        if not value or value.get("currency") != "USD":
+            raise ValueError("Missing/non-USD cash value")
+        return Decimal(value["value"])
+
+    def event_time(x):
+        if x.get("trade"):
+            t = x["trade"]
+            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t["isAggressor"] else t["passiveExecution"]
+            return e["transactTime"]
+        if x.get("positionResolution"):
+            return x["positionResolution"]["updateTime"]
+        return x["accountBalanceChange"]["createTime"]
+
+    def time_key(x):
+        # Preserve nanoseconds, including when an ISO timestamp has no fractional part.
+        at = event_time(x).removesuffix("Z")
+        seconds, _, fraction = at.partition(".")
+        return seconds, fraction.ljust(9, "0")
 
+    positions, pools, results, meta = {}, {}, {}, {}
+    owner_fees = {"ai": zero, "hand": zero}
+    funding = dict.fromkeys(("dep", "wd", "bonus", "transfer"), zero)
+    cash = charges = rebates = zero
+    seen, events = set(), []
+    for x in sorted(acts, key=time_key):
+        at, fee, allocations = event_time(x), zero, {}
+        slug, tx = "", ""
+        if x.get("trade"):
+            t = x["trade"]
+            slug, rid, kind = t["marketSlug"], t["id"], "fill"
+            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t["isAggressor"] else t["passiveExecution"]
+            order = e["order"]
+            owners = {"MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_AUTOMATIC": "ai", "MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_MANUAL": "hand"}
+            if order.get("manualOrderIndicator") not in owners:
+                raise ValueError("Unknown fill owner: " + rid)
+            owner = owners[order["manualOrderIndicator"]]
+            q = Decimal(e.get("lastShares") or t["qtyDecimal"])
+            if order["side"] not in ("ORDER_SIDE_BUY", "ORDER_SIDE_SELL") or q <= zero:
+                raise ValueError("Invalid fill side/quantity: " + rid)
+            dq = q if order["side"] == "ORDER_SIDE_BUY" else -q
+            old = positions.get(slug, zero)
+            new = old + dq
+            fee = usd(e["commissionNotionalCollected"])
+            # Signed YES coordinates: lock/release SHORT collateral, even across zero.
+            delta = -dq * usd(e["lastPx"]) - max(-new, zero) + max(-old, zero) - fee
+            allocations[owner] = delta  # Every fill's cash, including its fee, belongs to its actor.
+            owner_fees[owner] += fee
+            charges += max(fee, zero)
+            rebates += max(-fee, zero)
+            pool = pools.setdefault(slug, {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)})
+            # Mixed-market convention: closing fills consume remaining shares proportionally
+            # from both owners. Only the excess of a crossing fill opens shares for its actor.
+            # Settlement pays the resulting remaining-share pool, never gross traded volume.
+            if old * dq < zero:
+                closing = min(abs(old), q)
+                remaining = Fraction(abs(old) - closing) / Fraction(abs(old))
+                pool["ai"] *= remaining
+                pool["hand"] *= remaining
+                opening = q - closing
+            else:
+                opening = q
+            pool[owner] += Fraction(opening)
+            positions[slug] = new
+            title = (t.get("market") or {}).get("question") or (order.get("marketMetadata") or {}).get("title") or slug
+        elif x.get("positionResolution"):
+            r = x["positionResolution"]
+            slug, kind = r["marketSlug"], "payout"
+            rid = slug + "@" + at
+            before = r["beforePosition"]
+            if positions.get(slug, zero) != Decimal(before["netPositionDecimal"]):
+                raise ValueError("Incomplete position history: " + slug)
+            delta = usd(before["cashValue"])  # Includes neutral payouts; realized is not cash.
+            pool = pools.get(slug, {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)})
+            total = sum(pool.values())
+            if not total and delta:
+                raise ValueError("Payout without owned shares: " + slug)
+            if total:
+                ratio = pool["ai"] / total
+                # Round only the ownership allocation to 1e-8 USD. The hand complement
+                # preserves the actual payout exactly, including repeating pool fractions.
+                ai_cash = (delta * Decimal(ratio.numerator) / Decimal(ratio.denominator)).quantize(Decimal("0.00000001"))
+                if pool["ai"]:
+                    allocations["ai"] = ai_cash
+                if pool["hand"]:
+                    allocations["hand"] = delta - ai_cash
+            positions[slug] = zero
+            pools[slug] = {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)}
+            title = (r.get("market") or {}).get("question") or (before.get("marketMetadata") or {}).get("title") or slug
+        elif x.get("accountBalanceChange"):
+            b = x["accountBalanceChange"]
+            rid = tx = b["transactionId"]
+            kind = {"ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT": "dep", "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_WITHDRAWAL": "wd",
+                    "ACTIVITY_TYPE_REFERRAL_BONUS": "bonus", "ACTIVITY_TYPE_TRANSFER": "transfer"}.get(x["type"])
+            if not kind:
+                raise ValueError("Unknown funding type: " + x["type"])
+            amount, status = usd(b["amount"]), b["status"]
+            pending = status == "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_PENDING"
+            if pending and kind == "dep":
+                advances = {t["transactionId"]: usd(t["advancedAmount"]) for t in b.get("transactions", []) if t.get("advancedAmount")}
+                amount = min(amount, sum(advances.values(), zero))
+                if amount <= zero:
+                    continue
+            elif status != "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_COMPLETED":
+                continue
+            # TRANSFER amounts in this export are signed credits; purpose is unspecified.
+            delta = -amount if kind == "wd" else amount
+            funding[kind] += amount
+            title = {"dep": "Deposit", "wd": "Withdrawal", "bonus": "Promotion bonus", "transfer": "Transfer credit"}[kind]
+            if pending:
+                title += " (advanced funds; deposit pending)"
+        else:
+            raise ValueError("Unknown activity type")
+        key = (kind, rid)
+        if key in seen:
+            raise ValueError("Duplicate activity; reconcile its versions: " + rid)
+        seen.add(key)
+        if slug:
+            market = results.setdefault(slug, {})
+            for owner, value in allocations.items():
+                market[owner] = market.get(owner, zero) + value
+            meta[slug] = (at, title)
+        cash += delta
+        ev = {"at": at, "k": kind, "usd": float(delta), "usd_exact": str(delta),
+              "cash_balance": float(cash), "cash_balance_exact": str(cash), "title": title,
+              "slug": slug, "fee_usd": float(fee), "owners": {o: float(v) for o, v in allocations.items()}}
+        if tx:
+            ev["tx"] = tx
+        events.append(ev)

+def pm_cash_ledger(acts):
+    """Replay actual USD movements, independently of realized-P&L snapshots or bot estimates."""
+    zero = Decimal(0)
+
+    def usd(value):
+        if not value or value.get("currency") != "USD":
+            raise ValueError("Missing/non-USD cash value")
+        return Decimal(value["value"])
 
+    def event_time(x):
+        if x.get("trade"):
+            t = x["trade"]
+            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t["isAggressor"] else t["passiveExecution"]
+            return e["transactTime"]
+        if x.get("positionResolution"):
+            return x["positionResolution"]["updateTime"]
+        return x["accountBalanceChange"]["createTime"]
 
+    def time_key(x):
+        # Preserve nanoseconds, including when an ISO timestamp has no fractional part.
+        at = event_time(x).removesuffix("Z")
+        seconds, _, fraction = at.partition(".")
+        return seconds, fraction.ljust(9, "0")
+
+    positions, pools, results, meta = {}, {}, {}, {}
+    owner_fees = {"ai": zero, "hand": zero}
+    funding = dict.fromkeys(("dep", "wd", "bonus", "transfer"), zero)
+    cash = charges = rebates = zero
+    seen, events = set(), []
+    for x in sorted(acts, key=time_key):
+        at, fee, allocations = event_time(x), zero, {}
+        slug, tx = "", ""
+        if x.get("trade"):
+            t = x["trade"]
+            slug, rid, kind = t["marketSlug"], t["id"], "fill"
+            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t["isAggressor"] else t["passiveExecution"]
+            order = e["order"]
+            owners = {"MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_AUTOMATIC": "ai", "MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_MANUAL": "hand"}
+            if order.get("manualOrderIndicator") not in owners:
+                raise ValueError("Unknown fill owner: " + rid)
+            owner = owners[order["manualOrderIndicator"]]
+            q = Decimal(e.get("lastShares") or t["qtyDecimal"])
+            if order["side"] not in ("ORDER_SIDE_BUY", "ORDER_SIDE_SELL") or q <= zero:
+                raise ValueError("Invalid fill side/quantity: " + rid)
+            dq = q if order["side"] == "ORDER_SIDE_BUY" else -q
+            old = positions.get(slug, zero)
+            new = old + dq
+            fee = usd(e["commissionNotionalCollected"])
+            # Signed YES coordinates: lock/release SHORT collateral, even across zero.
+            delta = -dq * usd(e["lastPx"]) - max(-new, zero) + max(-old, zero) - fee
+            allocations[owner] = delta  # Every fill's cash, including its fee, belongs to its actor.
+            owner_fees[owner] += fee
+            charges += max(fee, zero)
+            rebates += max(-fee, zero)
+            pool = pools.setdefault(slug, {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)})
+            # Mixed-market convention: closing fills consume remaining shares proportionally
+            # from both owners. Only the excess of a crossing fill opens shares for its actor.
+            # Settlement pays the resulting remaining-share pool, never gross traded volume.
+            if old * dq < zero:
+                closing = min(abs(old), q)
+                remaining = Fraction(abs(old) - closing) / Fraction(abs(old))
+                pool["ai"] *= remaining
+                pool["hand"] *= remaining
+                opening = q - closing
+            else:
+                opening = q
+            pool[owner] += Fraction(opening)
+            positions[slug] = new
+            title = (t.get("market") or {}).get("question") or (order.get("marketMetadata") or {}).get("title") or slug
+        elif x.get("positionResolution"):
+            r = x["positionResolution"]
+            slug, kind = r["marketSlug"], "payout"
+            rid = slug + "@" + at
+            before = r["beforePosition"]
+            if positions.get(slug, zero) != Decimal(before["netPositionDecimal"]):
+                raise ValueError("Incomplete position history: " + slug)
+            delta = usd(before["cashValue"])  # Includes neutral payouts; realized is not cash.
+            pool = pools.get(slug, {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)})
+            total = sum(pool.values())
+            if total != Fraction(abs(positions.get(slug, zero))):
+                raise ValueError("Owner shares do not match position: " + slug)
+            if not total and delta:
+                raise ValueError("Payout without owned shares: " + slug)
+            if total:
+                ratio = pool["ai"] / total
+                # Round only the ownership allocation to 1e-8 USD. The hand complement
+                # preserves the actual payout exactly, including repeating pool fractions.
+                ai_cash = delta if not pool["hand"] else zero if not pool["ai"] else (
+                    delta * Decimal(ratio.numerator) / Decimal(ratio.denominator)).quantize(Decimal("0.00000001"))
+                if pool["ai"]:
+                    allocations["ai"] = ai_cash
+                if pool["hand"]:
+                    allocations["hand"] = delta - ai_cash
+            positions[slug] = zero
+            pools[slug] = {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)}
+            title = (r.get("market") or {}).get("question") or (before.get("marketMetadata") or {}).get("title") or slug
+        elif x.get("accountBalanceChange"):
+            b = x["accountBalanceChange"]
+            rid = tx = b["transactionId"]
+            kind = {"ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT": "dep", "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_WITHDRAWAL": "wd",
+                    "ACTIVITY_TYPE_REFERRAL_BONUS": "bonus", "ACTIVITY_TYPE_TRANSFER": "transfer"}.get(x["type"])
+            if not kind:
+                raise ValueError("Unknown funding type: " + x["type"])
+            amount, status = usd(b["amount"]), b["status"]
+            pending = status == "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_PENDING"
+            if pending and kind == "dep":
+                advances = {t["transactionId"]: usd(t["advancedAmount"]) for t in b.get("transactions", []) if t.get("advancedAmount")}
+                amount = min(amount, sum(advances.values(), zero))
+                if amount <= zero:
+                    continue
+            elif status != "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_COMPLETED":
+                continue
+            # TRANSFER amounts in this export are signed credits; purpose is unspecified.
+            delta = -amount if kind == "wd" else amount
+            funding[kind] += amount
+            title = {"dep": "Deposit", "wd": "Withdrawal", "bonus": "Promotion bonus", "transfer": "Transfer credit"}[kind]
+            if pending:
+                title += " (advanced funds; deposit pending)"
+        else:
+            raise ValueError("Unknown activity type")
+        key = (kind, rid)
+        if key in seen:
+            raise ValueError("Duplicate activity; reconcile its versions: " + rid)
+        seen.add(key)
+        if slug:
+            assert sum(allocations.values(), zero) == delta
+            market = results.setdefault(slug, {})
+            for owner, value in allocations.items():
+                market[owner] = market.get(owner, zero) + value
+            meta[slug] = (at, title)
+        cash += delta
+        ev = {"at": at, "k": kind, "usd": float(delta), "usd_exact": str(delta),
+              "cash_balance": float(cash), "cash_balance_exact": str(cash), "title": title,
+              "slug": slug, "fee_usd": float(fee), "owners": {o: float(v) for o, v in allocations.items()}}
+        if tx:
+            ev["tx"] = tx
+        events.append(ev)
+
+    net_funding = funding["dep"] + funding["bonus"] + funding["transfer"] - funding["wd"]
+    out = {"deposits": float(funding["dep"]), "bonus": float(funding["bonus"]), "credits": float(funding["transfer"]),
+           "funding_in": float(net_funding + funding["wd"]), "withdrawals": float(funding["wd"]),
+           "net_funding": float(net_funding), "net_trading_cash": float(cash - net_funding), "fees_paid": float(charges - rebates),
+           "fee_charges": float(charges), "maker_rebates": float(rebates), "cash_balance": float(cash),
+           "cash_balance_exact": str(cash), "balance_kind": "cash", "events": events, "meta": meta,
+           "market_results": {s: {o: float(v) for o, v in m.items()} for s, m in results.items()},
+           "open_positions": {s: str(q) for s, q in positions.items() if q},
+           "ownership_convention": "Fills to their actor; remaining shares pooled proportionally for closes and settlement.",
+           "bot_allocation": "Approximate: each market's AI cash result split by absolute desk fill spend."}
+    for owner in ("ai", "hand"):
+        values = [m[owner] for m in results.values() if owner in m]
+        closed = [m[owner] for s, m in results.items() if owner in m and not positions[s]]
+        out[owner + "_result"] = float(sum(values, zero))
+        out[owner + "_result_exact"] = str(sum(values, zero))
+        out[owner + "_fees_paid"] = float(owner_fees[owner])
+        out[owner + "_markets"] = len(values)
+        out[owner + "_won"] = float(sum((v for v in closed if v > zero), zero))
+        out[owner + "_lost"] = float(-sum((v for v in closed if v < zero), zero))
+        out[owner + "_won_n"] = sum(v > zero for v in closed)
+        out[owner + "_lost_n"] = sum(v < zero for v in closed)
+    assert Decimal(out["ai_result_exact"]) + Decimal(out["hand_result_exact"]) + net_funding == cash
+    return out
+
+
 def apply_receipts(events):
     """Optional receipts (public.desk_receipts): a withdrawal with a receipt becomes a 'bill' event (yellow on the page).
     Match by Polymarket transaction id, else by day + amount. Only the bill name, amount and an optional link go public."""

+# Cash-ledger fix verification — 2026-10-18
+
+Implemented in `publish-crew-copy.py` and `crew-index-copy.html`. The publisher was **not run**. Tests extracted selected function definitions with Python's AST and called them directly against `polymarket-activity.json`; external dependencies were replaced with local stubs for integration checks. No account, database, network, or live-site action was performed.
+
+## Totals on the supplied export
+
+| Component | Exact USD |
+|---|---:|
+| Deposits, including the documented $75 pending advance | +318.67000000 |
+| Promotion bonus | +25.00000000 |
+| Transfer credits (purpose unspecified) | +5.07000000 |
+| Withdrawals | -44.50000000 |
+| **Net funding** | **+304.24000000** |
+| **AI trades, net after fees** | **-110.42415000** |
+| **Jaron by hand, net after fees** | **-125.01585000** |
+| **Final cash** | **68.80000000** |
+
+Exact reconciliation:
+
+```text
+318.67000000 + 25.00000000 + 5.07000000 - 44.50000000
+             - 110.42415000 - 125.01585000 = 68.80000000
+
+304.24000000 funding - 235.44000000 trading cash = 68.80000000
+```
+
+The displayed cents also add up without a remainder:
+
+```text
+$318.67 + $25.00 + $5.07 - $44.50 - $110.42 - $125.02 = $68.80
+```
+
+The funding card shows **$348.74**, explicitly comprising $318.67 deposits, +$25.00 bonus and +$5.07 credits. The account equation ends at snapshot cash; current cash and open-position value are identified separately instead of being used to invent a balancing event.
+
+| Fees | USD |
+|---|---:|
+| Commissions charged | 164.97 |
+| Maker rebates | -6.35 |
+| **Fees paid, all time (net)** | **158.62** |
+| AI net fees | 63.64 |
+| Hand net fees | 94.98 |
+
+Fees are already included in the owner results and must not be subtracted again. Closed-market counts are AI **108 won / 150 lost**, hand **108 won / 74 lost**. Counts use each owner's net cash result on markets whose account position is closed. Results include all cash, including open-market cash; the remaining position is **0.0100 share** in `tec-mls-eastconf-2026-12-12-w-mia`. A cash debit while a position remains open is not a final economic loss.
+
+## Cincinnati–West Virginia
+
+Market: `aec-cfb-cin-wvir-2026-10-17`. Both purchases ultimately pay zero.
+
+| Fill | Owner | Actual cash, including execution fee |
+|---|---|---:|
+| `D5GA3MTG2ZRM` | AI / AUTOMATIC | -$10.16325 |
+| `D5GT1JNGYZRP` | Jaron / MANUAL | -$0.99990 |
+| Settlement payout | Both | $0.00000 |
+| **Combined result** | | **-$11.16315** |
+
+Thus the displayed results are **AI -$10.16, hand -$1.00**. The new code assigns each purchase debit and fee to its actual actor rather than weighting losses by gross share volume.
+
+## Checks performed
+
+- All **1,987 events** (1,709 fills, 262 payouts, 16 funding movements) were compared to the independent cash-ledger implementation already supplied in `REPORT.md`. **Every cash delta and every running balance matched exactly**, using Decimal values, not just the endpoint. Input records remained unchanged.
+- Net funding plus exact AI and hand totals equals $68.80. Owner fees sum to $158.62; charged commissions minus maker rebates also equals $158.62. All events retain unrounded cash and running-balance strings alongside JSON numbers.
+- Every settlement's reconstructed signed position matches `beforePosition.netPositionDecimal`; remaining owner shares match that position. Payouts use `beforePosition.cashValue`, including neutral settlements. Markets closed entirely by fills are included.
+- Synthetic cases verified proportional pooling after a partial close, both directions of crossing through zero, short collateral, negative maker commissions, and a non-binary neutral payout split in a repeating 1:2 ratio. Pool shares are exact fractions. Only mixed-owner payout allocation is rounded to 1e-8 USD; its hand complement preserves each actual payout exactly.
+- Synthetic pending deposits without documented advances are excluded. Partial advances count only their advanced amount, nested transaction IDs are counted once, and pending withdrawals are excluded. Duplicate events, incomplete settlement positions and unknown fill owners are rejected.
+- `apply_polymarket` was called with local history and receipt stubs. It preserved all ledger events and exact headline totals, serialized the snapshot to JSON, removed transaction identifiers and retained the existing per-bot spend weights. The actual receipt matcher was separately exercised with a local stub: it relabeled a withdrawal as a bill without changing its amount or running balance.
+- The full HTML module passed JavaScript syntax and execution checks with the actual computed ledger and DOM stubs. Assertions checked all new card labels and amounts, the displayed cents equation, all **1,987 plotted events**, first/last slider and click selection, receipt links, escaped titles, absence of NaN coordinates, and the guard against showing old resolution-based snapshots as cash. A deliberately different live cash/open-position reading did not change the snapshot equation or chart endpoint.
+
+The mixed-market convention is documented in the code: every fill's cash belongs to its actor; closing fills consume both owners' remaining shares proportionally; a crossing fill opens only its excess for its actor; settlement pays the remaining share pool. This is an attribution convention, not exchange-provided owner lot identification.
+
+Per-bot allocation remains approximate, as requested: each market's AI result is split by absolute `desk_trades` fill spend. Existing independent cent rounding is preserved. In the synthetic 3:1 split, bot totals round to -$82.82 and -$27.61 (sum -$110.43), while the account headline remains -$110.42. **Those bot estimates never enter the account cash ledger.** Actual per-bot attribution cannot be verified without the unavailable live desk rows.
+
+Browser layout and the live site were not exercised; the shared `crew.js` and styles are not supplied here. The HTML copy implements its own cash chart so its event types and endpoint do not depend on the shared settled-P&L chart. Nothing was published.
+
+## Reproduce the exact ledger comparison locally
+
+Run this PowerShell snippet in this folder. It extracts pure functions, so it does not import `executor`, refresh history, call `main`, or run the publisher:
+
+```powershell
+@'
+import ast, json, re
+from decimal import Decimal as D
+from fractions import Fraction
+
+path = 'publish-crew-copy.py'
+tree = ast.parse(open(path, encoding='utf-8').read())
+names = {'pm_cash_ledger', 'pm_account_story', 'pm_realized_ai'}
+ns = {'Decimal': D, 'Fraction': Fraction}
+pure = ast.Module(body=[n for n in tree.body
+    if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in names], type_ignores=[])
+exec(compile(pure, path, 'exec'), ns)
+acts = json.load(open('polymarket-activity.json', encoding='utf-8'))
+s = ns['pm_account_story'](acts)
+
+report = open('REPORT.md', encoding='utf-8').read()
+code = re.search(r'```python\n(def pm_cash_ledger.*?\n)```', report, re.S).group(1)
+ref = {}
+exec(code, ref)
+expected = ref['pm_cash_ledger'](acts)
+running = D(0)
+for event, check in zip(s['events'], expected['events'], strict=True):
+    running += D(event['usd_exact'])
+    assert D(event['usd_exact']) == D(check['usd_exact'])
+    assert running == D(event['cash_balance_exact']) == D(check['cash_balance_exact'])
+assert len(s['events']) == 1987
+assert running == D('68.80')
+assert D(s['ai_result_exact']) + D(s['hand_result_exact']) + D(str(s['net_funding'])) == running
+assert D(str(s['ai_fees_paid'])) + D(str(s['hand_fees_paid'])) == D('158.62')
+assert D(str(s['fee_charges'])) - D(str(s['maker_rebates'])) == D('158.62')
+assert s['market_results']['aec-cfb-cin-wvir-2026-10-17'] == {'ai': -10.16325, 'hand': -0.9999}
+print('PASS: 1,987 deltas and running balances; $68.80 cash; owner totals; fees; Cincinnati.')
+'@ | python -
+```

+  // This cash chart lives here because the shared crew.js chart expects settled P&L events.
+  // Plot the ledger balances without appending a live portfolio value or rounding each fill.
+  function mountCashChart(el, events) {
+    if (!events.length) {
+      el.innerHTML = '<p class="muted small">Waiting for a cash-ledger snapshot.</p>';
+      return;
+    }
+    const W = 900, H = 300, left = 65, right = 20, top = 20, bottom = 35;
+    const start = Date.parse(events[0].at), end = Date.parse(events[events.length - 1].at);
+    const balances = [0, ...events.map((e) => Number(e.cash_balance))];
+    const low = Math.min(...balances), high = Math.max(...balances), span = high - low || 1;
+    const x = (at) => left + (Date.parse(at) - start) / (end - start || 1) * (W - left - right);
+    const y = (value) => H - bottom - (value - low) / span * (H - top - bottom);
+    const color = (e) => e.k === "bill" ? "#f2c94c" : ["dep", "bonus", "transfer"].includes(e.k) ? "#b07cff"
+      : e.k === "wd" || Object.hasOwn(e.owners || {}, "hand") ? "#4c9aff" : e.usd < 0 ? "#e23a45" : "#3fb950";
+    // Horizontal, then vertical: cash changes only at an activity, not between activities.
+    const path = `M${left},${y(0)} ` + events.map((e) => `H${x(e.at)} V${y(e.cash_balance)}`).join(" ");
+    const ticks = Array.from({length: 5}, (_, i) => {
+      const v = low + span * i / 4, yy = y(v);
+      return `<line x1="${left}" x2="${W - right}" y1="${yy}" y2="${yy}" stroke="#293442"/><text x="${left - 8}" y="${yy + 4}" fill="#aab6c2" text-anchor="end" font-size="12">${esc(usd(v))}</text>`;
+    }).join("");
+    el.classList.add("cashchart");
+    el.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" role="img" aria-label="Running account cash from zero through every recorded activity">
+      ${ticks}<path d="${path}" fill="none" stroke="#c2ccd6" stroke-width="1.5"/>
+      ${events.map((e, i) => `<circle data-event="${i}" cx="${x(e.at)}" cy="${y(e.cash_balance)}" r="2.5" fill="${color(e)}"><title>${esc(e.title)}: ${esc(sg(e.usd))}; cash ${esc(usd(e.cash_balance))}</title></circle>`).join("")}
+      <circle id="cashselected" r="5" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width="2"/>
+      <text x="${left}" y="${H - 8}" fill="#aab6c2" font-size="12">${esc(events[0].at.slice(0, 10))}</text>
+      <text x="${W - right}" y="${H - 8}" fill="#aab6c2" text-anchor="end" font-size="12">${esc(events[events.length - 1].at.slice(0, 10))}</text>
+      </svg><label for="cashevent">Inspect cash activity</label><input id="cashevent" type="range" min="0" max="${events.length - 1}" value="${events.length - 1}" step="1"/><p class="cashdetail" aria-live="polite"></p>`;
+    const slider = el.querySelector("input"), detail = el.querySelector(".cashdetail"), marker = el.querySelector("#cashselected");
+    const show = (i) => {
+      const e = events[i];
+      slider.value = i;
+      marker.setAttribute("cx", x(e.at));
+      marker.setAttribute("cy", y(e.cash_balance));
+      const owners = Object.entries(e.owners || {}).map(([o, v]) => `${o === "ai" ? "AI" : "Jaron by hand"} ${sg(v)}`).join(", ");
+      detail.textContent = `${i + 1} / ${events.length} · ${e.at} · ${e.k}: ${e.title}. Cash move ${sg(e.usd)}; balance ${usd(e.cash_balance)}.${owners ? " " + owners + "." : ""}${e.fee_usd ? " Net fee " + usd(e.fee_usd) + "." : ""}${e.bill ? " Bill: " + e.bill + "." : ""}`;
+      // Match the existing receipt feature, accepting only web links.
+      if (e.receipt && /^https?:\/\//.test(e.receipt)) {
+        const link = document.createElement("a");
+        link.href = e.receipt;
+        link.textContent = " View receipt";
+        detail.append(link);
+      }

+def pm_account_story(acts, ai_by_slug=None):
+    """Account and owner cash results; the legacy ai_by_slug estimate is no longer used."""
+    return pm_cash_ledger(acts)
+
+
+def pm_cash_ledger(acts):
+    """Replay actual USD movements, independently of realized-P&L snapshots or bot estimates."""
+    zero = Decimal(0)
+
+    def usd(value):
+        if not value or value.get("currency") != "USD":
+            raise ValueError("Missing/non-USD cash value")
+        return Decimal(value["value"])
 
+    def event_time(x):
+        if x.get("trade"):
+            t = x["trade"]
+            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t["isAggressor"] else t["passiveExecution"]
+            return e["transactTime"]
+        if x.get("positionResolution"):
+            return x["positionResolution"]["updateTime"]
+        return x["accountBalanceChange"]["createTime"]
 
+    def time_key(x):
+        # Preserve nanoseconds, including when an ISO timestamp has no fractional part.
+        at = event_time(x).removesuffix("Z")
+        seconds, _, fraction = at.partition(".")
+        return seconds, fraction.ljust(9, "0")
+
+    positions, pools, results, meta = {}, {}, {}, {}
+    owner_fees = {"ai": zero, "hand": zero}
+    funding = dict.fromkeys(("dep", "wd", "bonus", "transfer"), zero)
+    cash = charges = rebates = zero
+    seen, events = set(), []
+    for x in sorted(acts, key=time_key):
+        at, fee, allocations = event_time(x), zero, {}
+        slug, tx = "", ""
+        if x.get("trade"):
+            t = x["trade"]
+            slug, rid, kind = t["marketSlug"], t["id"], "fill"
+            e = t["aggressorExecution"] if t["isAggressor"] else t["passiveExecution"]
+            order = e["order"]
+            owners = {"MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_AUTOMATIC": "ai", "MANUAL_ORDER_INDICATOR_MANUAL": "hand"}
+            if order.get("manualOrderIndicator") not in owners:
+                raise ValueError("Unknown fill owner: " + rid)
+            owner = owners[order["manualOrderIndicator"]]
+            q = Decimal(e.get("lastShares") or t["qtyDecimal"])
+            if order["side"] not in ("ORDER_SIDE_BUY", "ORDER_SIDE_SELL") or q <= zero:
+                raise ValueError("Invalid fill side/quantity: " + rid)
+            dq = q if order["side"] == "ORDER_SIDE_BUY" else -q
+            old = positions.get(slug, zero)
+            new = old + dq
+            fee = usd(e["commissionNotionalCollected"])
+            # Signed YES coordinates: lock/release SHORT collateral, even across zero.
+            delta = -dq * usd(e["lastPx"]) - max(-new, zero) + max(-old, zero) - fee
+            allocations[owner] = delta  # Every fill's cash, including its fee, belongs to its actor.
+            owner_fees[owner] += fee
+            charges += max(fee, zero)
+            rebates += max(-fee, zero)
+            pool = pools.setdefault(slug, {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)})
+            # Mixed-market convention: closing fills consume remaining shares proportionally
+            # from both owners. Only the excess of a crossing fill opens shares for its actor.
+            # Settlement pays the resulting remaining-share pool, never gross traded volume.
+            if old * dq < zero:
+                closing = min(abs(old), q)
+                remaining = Fraction(abs(old) - closing) / Fraction(abs(old))
+                pool["ai"] *= remaining
+                pool["hand"] *= remaining
+                opening = q - closing
+            else:
+                opening = q
+            pool[owner] += Fraction(opening)
+            positions[slug] = new
+            title = (t.get("market") or {}).get("question") or (order.get("marketMetadata") or {}).get("title") or slug
+        elif x.get("positionResolution"):
+            r = x["positionResolution"]
+            slug, kind = r["marketSlug"], "payout"
+            rid = slug + "@" + at
+            before = r["beforePosition"]
+            if positions.get(slug, zero) != Decimal(before["netPositionDecimal"]):
+                raise ValueError("Incomplete position history: " + slug)
+            delta = usd(before["cashValue"])  # Includes neutral payouts; realized is not cash.
+            pool = pools.get(slug, {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)})
+            total = sum(pool.values())
+            if total != Fraction(abs(positions.get(slug, zero))):
+                raise ValueError("Owner shares do not match position: " + slug)
+            if not total and delta:
+                raise ValueError("Payout without owned shares: " + slug)
+            if total:
+                ratio = pool["ai"] / total
+                # Round only the ownership allocation to 1e-8 USD. The hand complement
+                # preserves the actual payout exactly, including repeating pool fractions.
+                ai_cash = delta if not pool["hand"] else zero if not pool["ai"] else (
+                    delta * Decimal(ratio.numerator) / Decimal(ratio.denominator)).quantize(Decimal("0.00000001"))
+                if pool["ai"]:
+                    allocations["ai"] = ai_cash
+                if pool["hand"]:
+                    allocations["hand"] = delta - ai_cash
+            positions[slug] = zero
+            pools[slug] = {"ai": Fraction(0), "hand": Fraction(0)}
+            title = (r.get("market") or {}).get("question") or (before.get("marketMetadata") or {}).get("title") or slug
+        elif x.get("accountBalanceChange"):
+            b = x["accountBalanceChange"]
+            rid = tx = b["transactionId"]
+            kind = {"ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT": "dep", "ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_WITHDRAWAL": "wd",
+                    "ACTIVITY_TYPE_REFERRAL_BONUS": "bonus", "ACTIVITY_TYPE_TRANSFER": "transfer"}.get(x["type"])
+            if not kind:
+                raise ValueError("Unknown funding type: " + x["type"])
+            amount, status = usd(b["amount"]), b["status"]
+            pending = status == "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_PENDING"
+            if pending and kind == "dep":
+                advances = {t["transactionId"]: usd(t["advancedAmount"]) for t in b.get("transactions", []) if t.get("advancedAmount")}
+                amount = min(amount, sum(advances.values(), zero))
+                if amount <= zero:
+                    continue
+            elif status != "ACCOUNT_BALANCE_CHANGE_STATUS_COMPLETED":
+                continue
+            # TRANSFER amounts in this export are signed credits; purpose is unspecified.
+            delta = -amount if kind == "wd" else amount
+            funding[kind] += amount
+            title = {"dep": "Deposit", "wd": "Withdrawal", "bonus": "Promotion bonus", "transfer": "Transfer credit"}[kind]
+            if pending:
+                title += " (advanced funds; deposit pending)"
+        else:
+            raise ValueError("Unknown activity type")
+        key = (kind, rid)
+        if key in seen:
+            raise ValueError("Duplicate activity; reconcile its versions: " + rid)
+        seen.add(key)
+        if slug:
+            assert sum(allocations.values(), zero) == delta
+            market = results.setdefault(slug, {})
+            for owner, value in allocations.items():
+                market[owner] = market.get(owner, zero) + value
+            meta[slug] = (at, title)
+        cash += delta
+        ev = {"at": at, "k": kind, "usd": float(delta), "usd_exact": str(delta),
+              "cash_balance": float(cash), "cash_balance_exact": str(cash), "title": title,
+              "slug": slug, "fee_usd": float(fee), "owners": {o: float(v) for o, v in allocations.items()}}
+        if tx:
+            ev["tx"] = tx
+        events.append(ev)
+
+    net_funding = funding["dep"] + funding["bonus"] + funding["transfer"] - funding["wd"]
+    out = {"deposits": float(funding["dep"]), "bonus": float(funding["bonus"]), "credits": float(funding["transfer"]),
+           "funding_in": float(net_funding + funding["wd"]), "withdrawals": float(funding["wd"]),
+           "net_funding": float(net_funding), "net_trading_cash": float(cash - net_funding), "fees_paid": float(charges - rebates),
+           "fee_charges": float(charges), "maker_rebates": float(rebates), "cash_balance": float(cash),
+           "cash_balance_exact": str(cash), "balance_kind": "cash", "events": events, "meta": meta,
+           "market_results": {s: {o: float(v) for o, v in m.items()} for s, m in results.items()},
+           "open_positions": {s: str(q) for s, q in positions.items() if q},
+           "ownership_convention": "Fills to their actor; remaining shares pooled proportionally for closes and settlement.",
+           "bot_allocation": "Approximate: each market's AI cash result split by absolute desk fill spend."}
+    for owner in ("ai", "hand"):
+        values = [m[owner] for m in results.values() if owner in m]
+        closed = [m[owner] for s, m in results.items() if owner in m and not positions[s]]
+        out[owner + "_result"] = float(sum(values, zero))
+        out[owner + "_result_exact"] = str(sum(values, zero))
+        out[owner + "_fees_paid"] = float(owner_fees[owner])
+        out[owner + "_markets"] = len(values)
+        out[owner + "_won"] = float(sum((v for v in closed if v > zero), zero))
+        out[owner + "_lost"] = float(-sum((v for v in closed if v < zero), zero))
+        out[owner + "_won_n"] = sum(v > zero for v in closed)
+        out[owner + "_lost_n"] = sum(v < zero for v in closed)
+    assert Decimal(out["ai_result_exact"]) + Decimal(out["hand_result_exact"]) + net_funding == cash
+    return out
+
+

def apply_receipts(events):
    """Optional receipts (public.desk_receipts): a withdrawal with a receipt becomes a 'bill' event (yellow on the page).
    Match by Polymarket transaction id, else by day + amount. Only the bill name, amount and an optional link go public."""
    try:
        env = X.secrets()
        recs = X.sb(env, "GET", "desk_receipts?select=tx_id,paid_on,amount_usd,what,receipt_url&order=id.asc&limit=500") or []
    except Exception as e:
        print("receipts unavailable:", type(e).__name__)
        return 0.0
    total, used = 0.0, set()
    for ev in events:
        if ev.get("k") != "wd":
            continue
        for i, r in enumerate(recs):
            if i in used:
                continue
            amt = float(r.get("amount_usd") or 0)
            same_tx = r.get("tx_id") and r["tx_id"] == ev.get("tx")
            same_day = (not r.get("tx_id")) and r.get("paid_on") and abs(amt - abs(ev["usd"])) < 0.005 and                 abs((datetime.fromisoformat(ev["at"][:10]) - datetime.fromisoformat(str(r["paid_on"])[:10])).days) <= 1
            if same_tx or same_day:
                used.add(i)
                ev["k"] = "bill"
                ev["bill"] = mask(str(r["what"]))[:80]
                ev["bill_usd"] = round(amt, 2)
                if r.get("receipt_url") and str(r["receipt_url"]).startswith("http"):
                    ev["receipt"] = r["receipt_url"]
                total += amt
                break
    for ev in events:
        ev.pop("tx", None)
    return round(total, 2)


def apply_polymarket(per, rows):
    """Use real owner cash for the headline; per-bot splitting by desk fill spend remains approximate."""
    acts = pm_history()
    if not acts:
        return None
    story = pm_account_story(acts)
    ai = {s: v["ai"] for s, v in story["market_results"].items() if "ai" in v}
    w = {}
    for r in rows:
        if r.get("kind") == "fill" and (r.get("actor") or "").startswith("auto:") and r.get("slug") in ai:
            a = era(r)
            w.setdefault(r["slug"], {})
            w[r["slug"]][a] = w[r["slug"]].get(a, 0.0) + abs(float(r.get("cost_usd") or 0))
    for p in per.values():
        p["pnl"], p["settled"], p["wins"], p["losses"] = 0.0, 0, 0, 0
    other = 0.0
    for slug, v in ai.items():
        ws = w.get(slug)
        tot = sum(ws.values()) if ws else 0
        if not tot:
            other += v
            continue
        for a, x in ws.items():
            part = v * x / tot
            per[a]["pnl"] += part
            per[a]["settled"] += 1
            per[a]["wins" if part > 0 else "losses"] += 1 if part != 0 else 0
    for p in per.values():
        p["pnl"] = round(p["pnl"], 2)
    story.pop("meta")
    story["bills_paid"] = apply_receipts(story["events"])
    for event in story["events"]:
        event.pop("tx", None)
    global ACCOUNT_STORY
    ACCOUNT_STORY = story
    # Keep ai_settled as a compatibility alias for existing readers; source describes its cash basis.
    return {"ai_result": story["ai_result"], "ai_settled": story["ai_result"],
            "fees_paid": story["fees_paid"], "ai_fees_paid": story["ai_fees_paid"], "hand_fees_paid": story["hand_fees_paid"],
            "unattributed": round(other, 2), "markets": len(ai),
            "source": "Polymarket fill cash after fees plus owned settlement payouts; per-bot allocation approximate"}

def money(env):
    rows = X.sb_all(env, "desk_trades?select=actor,kind,cost_usd,pnl_usd,at,slug,side")
    per = {}
    for r in rows:
        a = era(r)
        p = per.setdefault(a, {"fills": 0, "spent": 0.0, "settled": 0, "pnl": 0.0, "wins": 0, "losses": 0, "last": "", "_t": set()})
        if r.get("kind") == "fill":
            # one trade = one market + side (+ hour), however many partial fills the venue split it into
            p["_t"].add((r.get("slug"), r.get("side"), (r.get("at") or "")[:13]))
            p["spent"] += float(r.get("cost_usd") or 0)
        elif r.get("kind") == "resolution":
            v = float(r.get("pnl_usd") or 0)
